1 : 0;\n149\t    weakMatchCount += weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;\n150\t    const allowlisted = allowlist.has(source.relativePath);\n151\t    result.observations.push({\n152\t      id: `test-gap-source-file:${source.relativePath}`,\n153\t      type: 'test_gap_source_file',\n154\t      path: source.relativePath,\n155\t      line: 1,\n156\t      name: basename(source.relativePath),\n157\t      details: {\n158\t        riskClass: risk.riskClass,\n159\t        highRisk: risk.highRisk,\n160\t        securitySensitive: risk.securitySensitive,\n161\t        migrationHazard: risk.migrationHazard,\n162\t        writeBoundary: risk.writeBoundary,\n163\t        matchedTests: matchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n164\t        weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n165\t        allowlisted,\n166\t      },\n167\t    });\n168\t    if (allowlisted || matchedTests.length > 0) {\n169\t      continue;\n170\t    }\n171\t    if (risk.migrationHazard) {\n172\t      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n173\t      result.findings.push({\n174\t        id: `migration-without-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n175\t        rule: 'migration_without_test',\n176\t        severity: 'high',\n177\t        path: source.relativePath,\n178\t        message: 'Hazardous migration has no adjacent or importing test coverage signal.',\n179\t        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n180\t        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n182\t        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n198\t        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n199\t        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n200\t      });\n201\t      continue;\n202\t    }\n203\t    if (input.includeWriteBoundaryFindings === true && risk.highRisk && risk.writeBoundary) {\n204\t      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n205\t      result.findings.push({\n206\t        id: `high-risk-source-without-adjacent-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n207\t        rule: 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test',\n208\t        severity: 'medium',\n209\t        path: source.relativePath,\n210\t        message: 'High-risk source file has no adjacent, sibling __tests__, or importing test coverage signal.',\n211\t        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n212\t        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.5 : 0.75,\n213\t        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n214\t        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n215\t        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n216\t      });\n217\t    }\n218\t  }\n219\t\n220\t  result.observations.push({\n221\t    id: 'test-gap-coverage-summary',\n222\t    type: 'test_gap_coverage_summary',\n223\t    details: {\n224\t      sourceFiles: sources.length,\n225\t      testFiles: tests.length,\n226\t      directMatchCount,\n227\t      weakMatchCount,\n228\t      highRiskWithoutTest,\n229\t      unmatchedHighRiskCount: highRiskWithoutTest,\n230\t      scanMode: 'import_graph_v1',\n231\t    },\n232\t  });\n233\t\n234\t  result.observations.sort(compareById);\n235\t  result.findings.sort(compareById);\n236\t  result.readPaths.sort();\n237\t  const evidenceSources = Array.from(\n238\t    new Set(result.findings.flatMap((finding) => finding.evidence.map((evidence) => evidence.path))),\n239\t /dangerouslySetInnerHTML|DOMPurify|sanitizeHtml|@Public\\b|AuthGuard|TenantGuard|PermissionGuard/i.test(text)\n272\t    || /(\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\/auth\\/|\\/security\\/|\\/permission)/i.test(path);\n273\t  const writeBoundary = /@(Post|Put|Patch|Delete|Mutation)\\b|\\b(create|update|delete|remove|archive|approve|reject|reset|invite)\\w*\\s*\\(/i.test(text);\n274\t  const migrationHazard = /\\/migrations\\/.*\\.ts$/.test(path)\n275\t    && /\\b(DROP|DELETE|TRUNCATE|ALTER\\s+TYPE|ENABLE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY|FORCE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY)\\b/i.test(text);\n276\t  return { riskClass, highRisk, securitySensitive, migrationHazard, writeBoundary };\n277\t}\n278\t\n279\tfunction riskClassForPath(path: string): string {\n280\t  if (path.includes('/migrations/')) {\n281\t    return 'migration';\n282\t  }\n283\t  // match[1] is narrowed explicitly so the file stays clean under\n284\t  // --noUncheckedIndexedAccess (the capture group is structurally guaranteed,\n285\t  // but the index signature cannot prove it to the compiler).\n286\t  const match = path.match(/\\.([a-z-]+)\\.(ts|tsx)$/);\n287\t  if (match?.[1] !== undefined) {\n288\t    return match[1];\n289\t  }\n290\t  if (path.endsWith('.tsx')) {\n291\t    return 'ui_component';\n292\t  }\n293\t  return 'source';\n294\t}\n295\t\n296\tfunction matchingTests(\n297\t  source: FileUnit,\n298\t  tests: basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(source.relativePath);\n303\t  const sourceDir = dirname(source.relativePath);\n304\t  return tests.filter((test) => {\n305\t    const testBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(test.relativePath);\n306\t    if (testBase === sourceBase) {\n307\t      return true;\n308\t    }\n309\t    if (dirname(test.relativePath) === sourceDir && testBase.startsWith(sourceBase)) {\n310\t      return true;\n311\t    }\n312\t    if (dirname(test.relativePath).endsWith(`${sourceDir}/__tests__`) && testBase.includes(sourceBase)) {\n313\t      return true;\n314\t    }\n315\t    return importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).some((specifier) => {\n316\t      const resolved = resolveImportSpecifier(test.relativePath, specifier, sourcePaths, resolveImportSpecifier(\n348\t  importingPath: string,\n349\t  specifier: string,\n350\t  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n351\t  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n352\t): string | undefined {\n353\t  const candidates: string[] = [];\n354\t  if (specifier.startsWith('.')) {\n355\t    candidates.push(normalizePath(join(dirname(importingPath), specifier)));\n356\t  } else {\n357\t    for specifier.slice(alias.prefix.length, specifier.length - alias.suffix.length);\n362\t      for (const target of alias.targets) {\n363\t        candidates.push(target.replace('*', middle));\n364\t      }\n365\t    }\n366\t  }\n367\t  for (const candidate of candidates.flatMap(candidatePathVariants)) {\n368\t    if (sourcePaths.has(candidate)) {\n369\t      return candidate;\n370\t    }\n371\t  }\n372\t  return undefined;\n373\t}\n374\t\n375\tfunction candidatePathVariants(candidate: string): string[] {\n376\t  const normalized = normalizePath(candidate).replace(/^\\.\\//, '');\n377\t  return [\n378\t    normalized,\n379\t    `${normalized}.ts`,\n380\t    `${normalized}.tsx`,\n381\t    `${normalized}/index.ts`,\n382\t    `${normalized}/index.tsx`,\n383\t  ];\n384\t}\n385\t\n386\tfunction readPathAliases(workspaceRoot: string): readonly PathAlias[] {\n387\t  const path = resolve(workspaceRoot, 'tsconfig.base.json');\n388\t  if readonly string[]> };\n394\t    };\n395\t    return Object.entries(parsed.compilerOptions?.paths ?? {}).map(([key, targets]) => {\n396\t      const starIndex = key.indexOf('*');\n397\t      return {\n398\t        prefix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(0, starIndex) : key,\n399\t        suffix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(starIndex + 1) : '',\n400\t        targets: targets.map((target) => normalizePath(target)),\n401\t      };\n402\t    });\n403\t  } catch {\n404\t    return [];\n405\t  }\n406\t}\n407\t\n408\tfunction exportedSymbols(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n409\t  const symbols = new Set<string>();\n410\t  visit(sourceFile, (node) => {\n411\t    if ((ts.isClassDeclaration(node) || ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node) || ts.isInterfaceDeclaration(node) || ts.isTypeAliasDeclaration(node)) && node.name) {\n412\t      const modifiers = ts.canHaveModifiers(node) ? ts.getModifiers(node) ?? [] : [];\n413\t      if (modifiers.some((modifier) => modifier.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ExportKeyword)) {\n414\t        symbols.add(node.name.text);\n415\t      }\n416\t    }\n417\t  });\n418\t  return Array.from(symbols).sort();\n419\t}\n420\t\n421\tfunction readFileUnit(path: string, workspaceRoot: string): FileUnit {\n422\t  const text = readWorkspaceFile(path);\n423\t  return {\n424\t    path,\n425\t    relativePath: normalizePath(relative(workspaceRoot, path)),\n426\t    text,\n427\t    sourceFile: ts.createSourceFile(path, text, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true),\n428\t    isTest: isTestFile(path),\n429\t  };\n430\t}\n431\t\n432\tfunction collectSourceAndTestFiles(root: string): readonly string[] {\n433\t  return collectFiles(root, {\n434\t    extensions: ['.ts', '.tsx'],\n435\t    // E13 FP class (2): archived corpus (e.g. migrations/.archive/<timestamp>/)\n436\t    // is dead code — it can neither need test coverage nor PROVIDE it (an\n437\t    // archived spec must not satisfy a live source), so it is excluded from\n438\t    // the scan entirely rather than special-cased at finding time.\n439\t    includeFile: (name, path) => !name.endsWith('.d.ts') && !isArchivedWorkspacePath(path),\n440\t  });\n441\t}\n442\t\n443\tfunction isTestFile(path: string): boolean {\n444\t  const normalized = normalizePath(path);\n445\t  return /(__tests__|\\.spec\\.|\\.test\\.)/.test(normalized);\n446\t}\n447\t\n448\tfunction resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot: string, requestedPath: string): string {\n449\t  return resolveAdapterPath(workspaceRoot, requestedPath);\n450\t}\n451\t\n452\tfunction visit(node: ts.Node, visitor: (node: ts.Node) => void): void {\n453\t  visitor(node);\n454\t  ts.forEachChild(node, (child) => visit(child, visitor));\n455\t}\n456\t\n457\tfunction compareById(a: { readonly id: string }, b: { readonly id: string }): number {\n458\t  return a.id.localeCompare(b.id);\n459\t}\n460\t\n461\tfunction normalizePath(path: string): string {\n462\t  return normalizeWorkspacePath(path);\n463\t}\n464\t\n465\tfunction readStdin(): Promise<string> {\n466\t  return new Promise((resolvePromise, reject) => {\n467\t    let input = '';\n468\t    process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');\n469\t    // setEncoding('utf8') makes every chunk a string at runtime, but the\n470\t    // stream's declared chunk type stays `string | Buffer` — concatenating the\n471\t    // union is what the type checker rejects. Narrow at the boundary rather\n472\t    // than widening `input` (kernel-dead-wire-adapter is the converged shape).\n473\t    process.stdin.on('data', (chunk: string | Buffer) => {\n474\t      input += typeof chunk === 'string' ? chunk : chunk.toString('utf8');\n475\t    });\n476\t    process.stdin.on('end', () => resolvePromise(input));\n477\t    process.stdin.on('error', reject);\n478\t  });\n479\t}\n480\t\n481\tasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n482\t  const rawInput = await readStdin();\n483\t  const input = rawInput.trim().length > 0 ? (JSON.parse(rawInput) as AdapterInput) : {};\n484\t  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(analyzeTestGaps(input))}\\n`);\n485\t}\n486\t\n487\tif (require.main === module) {\n488\t  main().catch((error: unknown) => {\n489\t    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n490\t    process.stderr.write(`${message}\\n`);\n491\t    process.exit(1);\n492\t  });\n493\t}\n494\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d5296f0-6d3c-4867-b93d-0a25583fcc68","uuid":"c93d9bda-4be4-4c22-b6ce-0783ca168c01","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:34:46.562Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-0db550b56f62/tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts","content":"#!/usr/bin/env readonly string[];\n}\n\n\nexport function analyzeTestGaps(input: AdapterInput, workspaceRoot = process.cwd()): AriaOutput {\n  const roots = requireScanRoots('test-gap-adapter', input.roots);\n  const allowlist = new Set((input.allowlist ?? []).map(normalizePath));\n  const files = roots\n    .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n    .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n    .flatMap((root) => collectSourceAndTestFiles(root));\n  const snapshotFiles = filterFilesBySnapshot(files, workspaceRoot, input);\n  const units = snapshotFiles.map((file) => readFileUnit(file, workspaceRoot));\n  const tests = units.filter((unit) => unit.isTest);\n  const sources = units.filter((unit) => !unit.isTest);\n  const sourcePaths = new Set(sources.map((source) => source.relativePath));\n  const pathAliases = readPathAliases(workspaceRoot);\n  const result: AnalysisResult = {\n    observations: [],\n    findings: [],\n    readPaths: units.map((unit) => unit.relativePath),\n  };\n\n  for (const test of tests) {\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-test-file:${test.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_test_file',\n      path: test.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(test.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        imports: importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).sort(),\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  let highRiskWithoutTest = 0;\n  let directMatchCount = 0;\n  let weakMatchCount = 0;\n  for (const source of sources) {\n    const risk = classifySourceRisk(source);\n    if (!risk.highRisk && !risk.securitySensitive && !risk.migrationHazard) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    const matchedTests = matchingTests(source, tests, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n    const weakMatchedTests = weakSymbolTests(source, tests).filter(\n      (test) => !matchedTests.some((matched) => matched.relativePath === test.relativePath),\n    );\n    directMatchCount += matchedTests.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;\n    weakMatchCount += weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;\n    const allowlisted = allowlist.has(source.relativePath);\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-source-file:${source.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_source_file',\n      path: source.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(source.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        riskClass: risk.riskClass,\n        highRisk: risk.highRisk,\n        securitySensitive: risk.securitySensitive,\n        migrationHazard: risk.migrationHazard,\n        writeBoundary: risk.writeBoundary,\n        matchedTests: matchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n        weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n        allowlisted,\n      },\n    });\n    if (allowlisted || matchedTests.length > 0) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (risk.migrationHazard) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `migration-without-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'migration_without_test',\n        severity: 'high',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'Hazardous migration has no adjacent or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.55 : 0.88,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (risk.securitySensitive) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `security-source-without-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'security_source_without_security_test',\n        severity: 'high',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'Security-sensitive source file has no adjacent or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.55 : 0.86,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (input.includeWriteBoundaryFindings === true && risk.highRisk && risk.writeBoundary) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `high-risk-source-without-adjacent-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test',\n        severity: 'medium',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'High-risk source file has no adjacent, sibling __tests__, or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.5 : 0.75,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  result.observations.push({\n    id: 'test-gap-coverage-summary',\n    type: 'test_gap_coverage_summary',\n    details: {\n      sourceFiles: sources.length,\n      testFiles: tests.length,\n      directMatchCount,\n      weakMatchCount,\n      highRiskWithoutTest,\n      unmatchedHighRiskCount: highRiskWithoutTest,\n      scanMode: 'import_graph_v1',\n    },\n  });\n\n  result.observations.sort(compareById);\n  result.findings.sort(compareById);\n  result.readPaths.sort();\n  const evidenceSources = Array.from(\n    new Set(result.findings.flatMap((finding) => finding.evidence.map((evidence) => evidence.path))),\n  ).sort();\n\n  return {\n    observations: result.observations,\n    findings: result.findings,\n    read_paths: Array.from(new Set(result.readPaths)).sort(),\n    evidence_sources: evidenceSources,\n    belief_candidates: [\n      {\n        belief_id: 'test-gap:high-risk-source-coverage-surface',\n        claim: 'high-risk backend, web security, and migration surfaces can be mapped to adjacent or importing test coverage signals',\n        confidence: 0.75,\n        evidence_refs: ['apps/**/*.ts', 'libs/**/*.ts', 'platform/libs/**/*.ts', 'web/**/*.tsx'],\n        source_tool_id: 'test-gap-adapter',\n      },\n    ],\n    cost_units: Array.from(new Set(result.readPaths)).length,\n    metadata: {\n      adapter: 'test-gap-adapter',\n      roots: roots.map(String).sort(),\n      files_scanned: snapshotFiles.length,\n      findings_count: result.findings.length,\n      allowlist_count: allowlist.size,\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nfunction classifySourceRisk(unit: FileUnit): SourceRisk {\n  const path = unit.relativePath;\n  const text = unit.text;\n  const riskClass = riskClassForPath(path);\n  const highRisk = /(\\.controller|\\.resolver|\\.handler|\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\.middleware|\\.interceptor)\\.ts$/.test(path);\n  const securitySensitive = /dangerouslySetInnerHTML|DOMPurify|sanitizeHtml|@Public\\b|AuthGuard|TenantGuard|PermissionGuard/i.test(text)\n    || /(\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\/auth\\/|\\/security\\/|\\/permission)/i.test(path);\n  const writeBoundary = /@(Post|Put|Patch|Delete|Mutation)\\b|\\b(create|update|delete|remove|archive|approve|reject|reset|invite)\\w*\\s*\\(/i.test(text);\n  const migrationHazard = /\\/migrations\\/.*\\.ts$/.test(path)\n    && /\\b(DROP|DELETE|TRUNCATE|ALTER\\s+TYPE|ENABLE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY|FORCE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY)\\b/i.test(text);\n  return { riskClass, highRisk, securitySensitive, migrationHazard, writeBoundary };\n}\n\nfunction riskClassForPath(path: string): string {\n  if (path.includes('/migrations/')) {\n    return 'migration';\n  }\n  // match[1] is narrowed explicitly so the file stays clean under\n  // --noUncheckedIndexedAccess (the capture group is structurally guaranteed,\n  // but the index signature cannot prove it to the compiler).\n  const match = path.match(/\\.([a-z-]+)\\.(ts|tsx)$/);\n  if (match?.[1] !== undefined) {\n    return match[1];\n  }\n  if (path.endsWith('.tsx')) {\n    return 'ui_component';\n  }\n  return 'source';\n}\n\nfunction matchingTests(\n  source: FileUnit,\n  tests: readonly FileUnit[],\n  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const sourceBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(source.relativePath);\n  const sourceDir = dirname(source.relativePath);\n  return tests.filter((test) => {\n    const testBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(test.relativePath);\n    if (testBase === sourceBase) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath) === sourceDir && testBase.startsWith(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath).endsWith(`${sourceDir}/__tests__`) && testBase.includes(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    return importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).some((specifier) => {\n      const resolved = resolveImportSpecifier(test.relativePath, specifier, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n      return resolved === source.relativePath;\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction weakSymbolTests(source: FileUnit, tests: readonly FileUnit[]): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const symbolNames = exportedSymbols(source.sourceFile);\n  if (symbolNames.length === 0) {\n    return [];\n  }\n  return tests.filter((test) => symbolNames.some((symbolName) => symbolName.length > 2 && test.text.includes(symbolName)));\n}\n\nfunction basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(path: string): string {\n  return basename(path)\n    .replace(/\\.(spec|test)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(controller|resolver|handler|guard|strategy|middleware|interceptor|service|entity|module|component)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '');\n}\n\nfunction importedSpecifiers(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n  const imports: string[] = [];\n  for (const statement of sourceFile.statements) {\n    if (ts.isImportDeclaration(statement) && ts.isStringLiteral(statement.moduleSpecifier)) {\n      imports.push(statement.moduleSpecifier.text);\n    }\n  }\n  return imports;\n}\n\nfunction resolveImportSpecifier(\n  importingPath: string,\n  specifier: string,\n  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n): string | undefined {\n  const candidates: string[] = [];\n  if (specifier.startsWith('.')) {\n    candidates.push(normalizePath(join(dirname(importingPath), specifier)));\n  } else {\n    for (const alias of pathAliases) {\n      if (!specifier.startsWith(alias.prefix) || !specifier.endsWith(alias.suffix)) {\n        continue;\n      }\n      const middle = specifier.slice(alias.prefix.length, specifier.length - alias.suffix.length);\n      for (const target of alias.targets) {\n        candidates.push(target.replace('*', middle));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  for (const candidate of candidates.flatMap(candidatePathVariants)) {\n    if (sourcePaths.has(candidate)) {\n      return candidate;\n    }\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction candidatePathVariants(candidate: string): string[] {\n  const normalized = normalizePath(candidate).replace(/^\\.\\//, '');\n  return [\n    normalized,\n    `${normalized}.ts`,\n    `${normalized}.tsx`,\n    `${normalized}/index.ts`,\n    `${normalized}/index.tsx`,\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction readPathAliases(workspaceRoot: string): readonly PathAlias[] {\n  const path = resolve(workspaceRoot, 'tsconfig.base.json');\n  if (!workspacePathExists(path)) {\n    return [];\n  }\n  try {\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(readWorkspaceFile(path)) as {\n      compilerOptions?: { paths?: Record<string, readonly string[]> };\n    };\n    return Object.entries(parsed.compilerOptions?.paths ?? {}).map(([key, targets]) => {\n      const starIndex = key.indexOf('*');\n      return {\n        prefix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(0, starIndex) : key,\n        suffix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(starIndex + 1) : '',\n        targets: targets.map((target) => normalizePath(target)),\n      };\n    });\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n}\n\nfunction exportedSymbols(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n  const symbols = new Set<string>();\n  visit(sourceFile, (node) => {\n    if ((ts.isClassDeclaration(node) || ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node) || ts.isInterfaceDeclaration(node) || ts.isTypeAliasDeclaration(node)) && node.name) {\n      const modifiers = ts.canHaveModifiers(node) ? ts.getModifiers(node) ?? [] : [];\n      if (modifiers.some((modifier) => modifier.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ExportKeyword)) {\n        symbols.add(node.name.text);\n      }\n    }\n  });\n  return Array.from(symbols).sort();\n}\n\nfunction readFileUnit(path: string, workspaceRoot: string): FileUnit {\n  const text = readWorkspaceFile(path);\n  return {\n    path,\n    relativePath: normalizePath(relative(workspaceRoot, path)),\n    text,\n    sourceFile: ts.createSourceFile(path, text, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true),\n    isTest: isTestFile(path),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction collectSourceAndTestFiles(root: string): readonly string[] {\n  return collectFiles(root, {\n    extensions: ['.ts', '.tsx'],\n    // E13 FP class (2): archived corpus (e.g. migrations/.archive/<timestamp>/)\n    // is dead code — it can neither need test coverage nor PROVIDE it (an\n    // archived spec must not satisfy a live source), so it is excluded from\n    // the scan entirely rather than special-cased at finding time.\n    includeFile: (name, path) => !name.endsWith('.d.ts') && !isArchivedWorkspacePath(path),\n  });\n}\n\nfunction isTestFile(path: string): boolean {\n  const normalized = normalizePath(path);\n  return /(__tests__|\\.spec\\.|\\.test\\.)/.test(normalized);\n}\n\nfunction resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot: string, requestedPath: string): string {\n  return resolveAdapterPath(workspaceRoot, requestedPath);\n}\n\nfunction visit(node: ts.Node, visitor: (node: ts.Node) => void): void {\n  visitor(node);\n  ts.forEachChild(node, (child) => visit(child, visitor));\n}\n\nfunction compareById(a: { readonly id: string }, b: { readonly id: string }): number {\n  return a.id.localeCompare(b.id);\n}\n\nfunction normalizePath(path: string): string {\n  return normalizeWorkspacePath(path);\n}\n\nfunction readStdin(): Promise<string> {\n  return new Promise((resolvePromise, reject) => {\n    let input = '';\n    process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');\n    // setEncoding('utf8') makes every chunk a string at runtime, but the\n    // stream's declared chunk type stays `string | Buffer` — concatenating the\n    // union is what the type checker rejects. Narrow at the boundary rather\n    // than widening `input` (kernel-dead-wire-adapter is the converged shape).\n    process.stdin.on('data', (chunk: string | Buffer) => {\n      input += typeof chunk === 'string' ? chunk : chunk.toString('utf8');\n    });\n    process.stdin.on('end', () => resolvePromise(input));\n    process.stdin.on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  const rawInput = await readStdin();\n  const input = rawInput.trim().length > 0 ? (JSON.parse(rawInput) as AdapterInput) : {};\n  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(analyzeTestGaps(input))}\\n`);\n}\n\nif (require.main === module) {\n  main().catch((error: unknown) => {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    process.stderr.write(`${message}\\n`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n}\n","numLines":494,"startLine":1,"totalLines":494}}}

* Regex unit tests for SyncHrEntitiesToDb1786800000000's whitelist + idempotency.\n *\n * The migration's two helper predicates (`isValidatorRelevant` and\n * `makeIdempotent`) are inlined inside its `up()` method to keep the\n * migration self-contained. We re-declare the SAME regex/replacement\n * shapes here and assert each known log() output statement class\n * receives the expected verdict.\n *\n * If the migration's inline helpers are ever edited, these tests must\n * be updated to match — both copies are mirrored 1:1 by intent.\n *\n * Failure modes this test catches:\n *   - Whitelist accidentally allows ADD CONSTRAINT (deploy 5 regression)\n *   - Whitelist accidentally rejects ALTER COLUMN TYPE (uuid drift goes uncaught)\n *   - makeIdempotent fails to inject IF NOT EXISTS into ADD COLUMN\n *   - makeIdempotent corrupts schema-qualified table names\n */\n\n// `export {}` keeps strict-tsc treating this file as a MODULE so its\n// top-level declarations stay file-scoped (PROC-MEDIUM-010 invariant).\nexport {};\ndescribe('SyncHrEntitiesToDb1786800000000 — Phase L whitelist + idempotency', () => {\n  // Mirrors apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations/1786800000000-SyncHrEntitiesToDb.ts\n  // §6 isValidatorRelevant. Update BOTH if either changes.\n  const isValidatorRelevant = (sql: string): boolean => {\n    const t = sql.trim();\n    if (/^CREATE\\s+TYPE\\b/i.test(t)) return true;\n    if (/^CREATE\\s+TABLE\\b/i.test(t)) return true;\n    if (/^ALTER\\s+TABLE\\b[^;]*?\\bADD\\s+(?!CONSTRAINT\\b)\"/i.test(t)) return true;\n    // Phase L+: any ALTER COLUMN sub-action passes (TYPE / SET NOT NULL\n    // / DROP NOT NULL / SET DEFAULT / DROP DEFAULT). The trio\n    // DROP-DEFAULT → TYPE → SET-DEFAULT must stay together for PG's\n    // enum type changes to succeed (deploy 6 regression).\n    if (/^ALTER\\s+TABLE\\b[^;]*?\\bALTER\\s+COLUMN\\b/i.test(t)) return true;\n    return false;\n  };\n\n  // Mirrors §6b makeIdempotent. Update BOTH if either changes.\n  const makeIdempotent = (sql: string): string => {\n    let s = sql;\n    s = s.replace(/^CREATE\\s+TABLE\\s+\"/i, 'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"');\n    s = s.replace(\n      /(\\bALTER\\s+TABLE\\s+\"[^\"]+\"\\.\"[^\"]+\"\\s+)ADD\\s+\"/i,\n      '$1ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"',\n    );\n    if (/^CREATE\\s+TYPE\\b/i.test(s)) {\n      s = `DO $$ BEGIN ${s}; EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$`;\n    }\n    return s;\n  };\n\n  describe('isValidatorRelevant — KEEP cases', () => {\n    it.each([\n      [\n        'CREATE TABLE for missing table',\n        `CREATE TABLE \"hr\".\"hr_outbox\" (\"id\" BIGSERIAL NOT NULL, \"eventType\" varchar(100))`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'CREATE TYPE for enum',\n        `CREATE TYPE \"hr\".\"shifts_shifttype_enum\" AS ENUM ('regular', 'overtime')`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ADD column (TypeORM omits COLUMN keyword)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN TYPE (uuid mismatch fix)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ALTER COLUMN \"tenantId\" TYPE uuid USING \"tenantId\"::uuid`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN SET NOT NULL',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ALTER COLUMN \"tenantId\" SET NOT NULL`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN DROP DEFAULT (deploy 6 trio precondition)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"training_enrollments\" ALTER COLUMN \"status\" DROP DEFAULT`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN SET DEFAULT (deploy 6 trio postcondition)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"training_enrollments\" ALTER COLUMN \"status\" SET DEFAULT 'enrolled'`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN DROP NOT NULL (validator does not flag relaxed nullability)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"weekly_plans\" ALTER COLUMN \"notifiedAt\" DROP NOT NULL`,\n      ],\n    ])('keeps %s', (_label, sql) => {\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(true);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('isValidatorRelevant — SKIP cases (validator does not check these)', () => {\n    it.each([\n      [\n        'ADD CONSTRAINT PRIMARY KEY (deploy 5 failure mode)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"scheduling_settings\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"PK_8e3892ce17b3a09c6172d83423d\" PRIMARY KEY (\"tenantId\")`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ADD CONSTRAINT UNIQUE',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"shifts\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"UQ_shift_tenant_code\" UNIQUE (\"tenantId\", \"code\")`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ADD CONSTRAINT FOREIGN KEY',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"employees\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"FK_employee_department\" FOREIGN KEY (\"departmentId\") REFERENCES \"hr\".\"departments_hr\"(\"id\")`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ADD CONSTRAINT CHECK',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"leave_requests\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"CK_dates\" CHECK (\"startDate\" <= \"endDate\")`,\n      ],\n      ['CREATE INDEX', `CREATE INDEX \"IDX_payroll_tenant\" ON \"hr\".\"payrolls\" (\"tenantId\")`],\n      ['DROP TABLE', `DROP TABLE \"hr\".\"old_table\"`],\n      [\n        'DROP TYPE (deploy 4 failure mode)',\n        `DROP TYPE \"hr\".\"work_week_day\"`,\n      ],\n      ['DROP COLUMN', `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" DROP COLUMN \"earnings\"`],\n      [\n        'DROP CONSTRAINT',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"employees\" DROP CONSTRAINT \"FK_old_dept\"`,\n      ],\n      ['COMMENT ON COLUMN', `COMMENT ON COLUMN \"hr\".\"payrolls\".\"tenantId\" IS 'tenant id'`],\n    ])('skips %s', (_label, sql) => {\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('makeIdempotent', () => {\n    it('rewrites CREATE TABLE → CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS', () => {\n      const input = `CREATE TABLE \"hr\".\"hr_outbox\" (\"id\" BIGSERIAL NOT NULL)`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(\n        `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"hr\".\"hr_outbox\" (\"id\" BIGSERIAL NOT NULL)`,\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('rewrites ALTER TABLE … ADD \"col\" → ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"col\"', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`,\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('wraps CREATE TYPE in DO/EXCEPTION duplicate_object', () => {\n      const input = `CREATE TYPE \"hr\".\"shifts_shifttype_enum\" AS ENUM ('regular')`;\n      const out = makeIdempotent(input);\n      expect(out).toContain('DO $$ BEGIN');\n      expect(out).toContain(input);\n      expect(out).toContain('EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL');\n    });\n\n    it('does NOT touch ALTER COLUMN TYPE (no idempotent rewrite needed; PG is idempotent at type level)', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ALTER COLUMN \"tenantId\" TYPE uuid USING \"tenantId\"::uuid`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(input);\n    });\n\n    it('does NOT touch ALTER COLUMN SET NOT NULL', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ALTER COLUMN \"tenantId\" SET NOT NULL`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(input);\n    });\n\n    it('preserves schema-qualified table names through ADD COLUMN rewrite', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"tenant_abc123def456abc1\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"newCol\" varchar`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(\n        `ALTER TABLE \"tenant_abc123def456abc1\".\"payrolls\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"newCol\" varchar`,\n      );\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('end-to-end pipeline (whitelist + idempotency)', () => {\n    it('keeps and rewrites a missing-column ADD scenario', () => {\n      const sql = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`;\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(true);\n      expect(makeIdempotent(sql)).toBe(\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`,\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('rejects the deploy 5 PK conflict scenario before idempotency runs', () => {\n      const sql = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"scheduling_settings\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"PK_x\" PRIMARY KEY (\"tenantId\")`;\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it('rejects the deploy 4 DROP TYPE cascade scenario', () => {\n      const sql = `DROP TYPE \"hr\".\"work_week_day\"`;\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":188,"startLine":1,"totalLines":188}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01G7EV8UP4vbAz6bQPtxgwD9","type":"tool_result","content":"1:name: Database Migration Check\n4:# Database Migration Check — TypeORM Migration Validation\n8:# Fast, hermetic validation of the TypeORM migration files that actually\n13:#      `typeorm migration:run -d dist/apps/gateway-api/src/data-source.js`.\n15:#      file — migrations are registered as class references inside\n16:#      `TypeOrmModule.forRoot({ migrations: [...] })` and executed at\n17:#      service-boot time by `MigrationRunnerService`, not by the typeorm\n20:#   2. Run Flyway validate against `database/migrations/core/**` and\n21:#      `database/migrations/modules/**`. The Flyway tree is a\n24:#      `database/migrations/**` (every service registers its own\n25:#      TypeORM migration classes via TypeOrmModule.forRoot per the\n32:#      `database/migrations/core/V*.sql` carried 281–2491 bytes\n33:#      and every `database/migrations/modules/<svc>/V*.sql` carried\n37:#      grep for the old phrasing.\n55:# The real migration path in this codebase is\n56:# `apps/<service>/src/**/migrations/<timestamp>-<Name>.ts`, one class\n60:#   1. Filename convention — every migration file name must match\n64:#   3. Global timestamp uniqueness — no two migrations anywhere in the\n66:#      undefined ordering under TypeORM's migration runner.\n67:#   4. Syntax / type check — each migration file compiles under\n78:# Real integration-level migration validation — i.e. \"does the\n79:# migration actually run against a database\" — happens at deploy time\n80:# via `MigrationRunnerService.onApplicationBootstrap()` and the\n91:      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n94:      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n97:      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n101:      - 'apps/*/src/**/migrations/**'\n103:      - 'apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts'\n106:      - '.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml'\n109:  # init-script change merged through a different filename, or a\n121:  typeorm-migration-validation:\n142:      - name: Validate migration filename and class name conventions\n150:              echo \"::error file=$file::Invalid migration filename: '$base' (expected <13-digit-timestamp>-<PascalCaseName>.ts)\"\n167:          done < <(find apps -type f -name '[0-9]*-*.ts' -not -name '*.spec.ts' -not -name '*.test.ts' -path '*/migrations/*' -not -path '*/.archive/*' 2>/dev/null | sort)\n172:          echo \"All migration filenames and class names match convention.\"\n175:      # Check 3: no two migrations WITHIN A SINGLE SERVICE share a\n176:      # timestamp. Each service owns its own `migrations` tracking table\n177:      # (TypeORM runs migrations per DataSource, and every service\n179:      # migration lineage. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01VjgncNBmYS5g2gL8AQ6LwS","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t/**\n2\t * Bootstrap From Scratch\n3\t * ============================================================================\n4\t *\n5\t * Proves that the fresh-volume init pipeline — `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/*`\n6\t * + every service's TypeORM migration chain — produces a clean, complete\n7\t * platform schema when run against an empty Postgres.\n8\t *\n9\t * # Why this test exists\n10\t *\n11\t * The codebase's bootstrap pipeline silently broke when several\n12\t * `CREATE TABLE` migrations were squashed out of source. Production survived\n13\t * because nobody did a fresh-volume init for months — every existing\n14\t * environment had the squashed-out tables already on disk. The first\n15\t * fresh-volume deploy crashed at `1781100000000-ConvertTimestampToTimestamptz`\n16\t * trying to ALTER `auth.users.mfaLockedUntil` (a column whose CREATE was\n17\t * gone). That is the exact regression class this invariant guards against.\n18\t *\n19\t * # What it does\n20\t *\n21\t *   1. Spin up `timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16` (the production image, by\n22\t *      digest pinned to docker-compose.infra.yml).\n23\t *   2. Copy `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/` into\n24\t *      `/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/` — Postgres runs every script in\n25\t *      lexical order on first boot of an empty PGDATA volume.\n26\t *   3. Connect, then for every service that owns a TypeORM migration\n27\t *      directory, dynamically load every migration class and run them\n28\t *      through a per-service DataSource bound to that service's schema.\n29\t *   4. Assert schema-level invariants: schemas exist, the previously\n30\t *      missing baseline tables now exist, the previously missing\n31\t *      `auth.users` columns now exist, the sensor hypertable was\n32\t *      created, db-migrate can owner-align a real tenant's continuous\n33\t *      aggregates, and each service's migration ledger is non-empty\n34\t *      (proving the migrations actually ran).\n35\t *\n36\t * # When this test fails\n37\t *\n38\t *   - A migration that creates a baseline table was squashed →\n39\t *     restore the CREATE in the earliest service migration. (This is\n40\t *     the original regression class.)\n41\t *   - An init-script SQL file was deleted without an equivalent\n42\t *     migration → either restore the script OR write a migration that\n43\t *     creates the same tables idempotently.\n44\t *   - A new entity was added without a corresponding CREATE TABLE\n45\t *     migration AND without an init-script entry → add the migration.\n46\t *   - The init scripts assume a privilege the bootstrap POSTGRES_USER\n47\t *     does not have on a fresh container → fix the script to use\n48\t *     a less-privileged path or supply additional bootstrap material.\n49\t *\n50\t * # Why testcontainers (not the shared TestDatabase pool)\n51\t *\n52\t * The other e2e/integration specs assert state of an *already-running*\n53\t * database that the dev/CI infra brought up — they're checking\n54\t * application-level behaviour. This test asserts the bootstrap ITSELF\n55\t * works on an EMPTY volume. It MUST start with no PGDATA, run the init\n56\t * scripts, then run the migration chain. testcontainers is the only\n57\t * way to get that hermetic guarantee inside a Jest worker.\n58\t *\n59\t * # Runtime\n60\t *\n61\t * ~3-5 minutes on a cold runner: image pull + init-script execution +\n62\t * 100+ migrations across 13 services. CI gate runs only on PRs that\n63\t * touch `apps/*\\/src/**\\/migrations/**` or\n64\t * `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**` (see CI workflow\n65\t * `.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml` integration; if the gate\n66\t * is not yet wired, `npm run test:bootstrap` is the local entry point).\n67\t */\n68\t\n69\timport { readdirSync, statSync, existsSync } from 'fs';\n70\timport { createRequire } from 'module';\n71\timport { join, resolve } from 'path';\n72\t\n73\timport { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\n74\timport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\n75\timport { GenericContainer, type StartedTestContainer, Wait } from 'testcontainers';\n76\timport { DataSource, type MigrationInterface } from 'typeorm';\n77\t\n78\t// ADR-031 Platform Bootstrap Atom — runs the schema/role/extension/function/\n79\t// shared-table DDL contract that init-scripts USED to own pre-cutover. The\n80\t// init-scripts directory copied below now contains only `01-init-databases.sql`\n81\t// (initdb-only DB GRANTs). Schemas, roles, functions, and shared.* tables\n82\t// are created by the call to runPlatformBootstrap() below before any\n83\t// per-service migration loop runs.\n84\timport {\n85\t  runPlatformBootstrap,\n86\t  resolvePlatformBootstrapSqlDir,\n87\t} from '../platform-bootstrap.service';\n88\timport { ensureTenantSensorContinuousAggregateAuthority } from '../tenant-sensor-continuous-aggregate-authority';\n89\t\n90\t// Constructor type for the @Entity / migration classes this spec loads\n91\t// dynamically. TypeORM's DataSource `entities`/`migrations` options accept\n92\t// `Function`, but `@typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-function-type` (correctly)\n93\t// rejects the bare `Function` type; a precise construct signature is both\n94\t// assignable to TypeORM's `Function` slot AND keeps the collected values\n95\t// typed as classes rather than arbitrary callables.\n96\ttype EntityClass = new (...args: never[]) => object;\n97\t\n98\t// Synchronous module loader bound to this spec's location. `import`/`await\n99\t// import()` cannot be used here: loadMigrationClassesFromDir is invoked inside\n100\t// `it.each(...)` arguments that Jest evaluates synchronously at collection\n101\t// time, so the load must be synchronous. `createRequire(__filename)` resolves\n102\t// migration classes and the cross-package schema-drift-validator through\n103\t// ts-jest at runtime, so the static type-check graph never reaches across\n104\t// project rootDirs — the same dynamic-load pattern the db-migrate\n105\t// platform-bootstrap.integration.spec.ts relies on.\n106\tconst requireModule = createRequire(__filename);\n107\t\n108\t// Structured logger for the (rare) entity-load skip path — `console.*` is\n109\t// banned platform-wide; the NestJS Logger keeps the diagnostic on the same\n110\t// structured-logging path the runtime services use.\n111\tconst logger = new Logger('bootstrap-from-scratch');\n112\t\n113\t// Local narrowing helper. db-migrate has @typescript-eslint/no-non-null-assertion\n114\t// ENABLED (unlike the e2e project, whose assertDefined SSoT this replaces), so a\n115\t// bare `!` is banned here. assertDefined narrows `T | undefined` to `T` and fails\n116\t// loudly at the assertion site instead of surfacing as a cryptic\n117\t// \"cannot read properties of undefined\" further downstream.\n118\tfunction assertDefined<T>(value: T | null | undefined, message?: string): T {\n119\t  if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n120\t    throw new Error(message ?? 'assertDefined: expected a value but received null/undefined');"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d5296f0-6d3c-4867-b93d-0a25583fcc68","uuid":"8a5ec8ec-eb0e-4f78-a4fd-0b4baed0342a","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:35:55.798Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-0db550b56f62/apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Bootstrap From Scratch\n * ============================================================================\n *\n * Proves that the fresh-volume init pipeline — `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/*`\n * + every service's TypeORM migration chain — produces a clean, complete\n * platform schema when run against an empty Postgres.\n *\n * # Why this test exists\n *\n * The codebase's bootstrap pipeline silently broke when several\n * `CREATE TABLE` migrations were squashed out of source. Production survived\n * because nobody did a fresh-volume init for months — every existing\n * environment had the squashed-out tables already on disk. The first\n * fresh-volume deploy crashed at `1781100000000-ConvertTimestampToTimestamptz`\n * trying to ALTER `auth.users.mfaLockedUntil` (a column whose CREATE was\n * gone). That is the exact regression class this invariant guards against.\n *\n * # What it does\n *\n *   1. Spin up `timescale/timescaledb-ha:pg16` (the production image, by\n *      digest pinned to docker-compose.infra.yml).\n *   2. Copy `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/` into\n *      `/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/` — Postgres runs every script in\n *      lexical order on first boot of an empty PGDATA volume.\n *   3. Connect, then for every service that owns a TypeORM migration\n *      directory, dynamically load every migration class and run them\n *      through a per-service DataSource bound to that service's schema.\n *   4. Assert schema-level invariants: schemas exist, the previously\n *      missing baseline tables now exist, the previously missing\n *      `auth.users` columns now exist, the sensor hypertable was\n *      created, db-migrate can owner-align a real tenant's continuous\n *      aggregates, and each service's migration ledger is non-empty\n *      (proving the migrations actually ran).\n *\n * # When this test fails\n *\n *   - A migration that creates a baseline table was squashed →\n *     restore the CREATE in the earliest service migration. (This is\n *     the original regression class.)\n *   - An init-script SQL file was deleted without an equivalent\n *     migration → either restore the script OR write a migration that\n *     creates the same tables idempotently.\n *   - A new entity was added without a corresponding CREATE TABLE\n *     migration AND without an init-script entry → add the migration.\n *   - The init scripts assume a privilege the bootstrap POSTGRES_USER\n *     does not have on a fresh container → fix the script to use\n *     a less-privileged path or supply additional bootstrap material.\n *\n * # Why testcontainers (not the shared TestDatabase pool)\n *\n * The other e2e/integration specs assert state of an *already-running*\n * database that the dev/CI infra brought up — they're checking\n * application-level behaviour. This test asserts the bootstrap ITSELF\n * works on an EMPTY volume. It MUST start with no PGDATA, run the init\n * scripts, then run the migration chain. testcontainers is the only\n * way to get that hermetic guarantee inside a Jest worker.\n *\n * # Runtime\n *\n * ~3-5 minutes on a cold runner: image pull + init-script execution +\n * 100+ migrations across 13 services. CI gate runs only on PRs that\n * touch `apps/*\\/src/**\\/migrations/**` or\n * `infrastructure/docker/init-scripts/**` (see CI workflow\n * `.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml` integration; if the gate\n * is not yet wired, `npm run test:bootstrap` is the local entry point).\n */\n\nimport { readdirSync, statSync, existsSync } from 'fs';\nimport { createRequire } from 'module';\nimport { join, resolve } from 'path';\n\nimport { Logger } from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';\nimport { GenericContainer, type StartedTestContainer, Wait } from 'testcontainers';\nimport { DataSource, type MigrationInterface } from 'typeorm';\n\n// ADR-031 Platform Bootstrap Atom — runs the schema/role/extension/function/\n// shared-table DDL contract that init-scripts USED to own pre-cutover. The\n// init-scripts directory copied below now contains only `01-init-databases.sql`\n// (initdb-only DB GRANTs). Schemas, roles, functions, and shared.* tables\n// are created by the call to runPlatformBootstrap() below before any\n// per-service migration loop runs.\nimport {\n  runPlatformBootstrap,\n  resolvePlatformBootstrapSqlDir,\n} from '../platform-bootstrap.service';\nimport { ensureTenantSensorContinuousAggregateAuthority } from '../tenant-sensor-continuous-aggregate-authority';\n\n// Constructor type for the @Entity / migration classes this spec loads\n// dynamically. TypeORM's DataSource `entities`/`migrations` options accept\n// `Function`, but `@typescript-eslint/no-unsafe-function-type` (correctly)\n// rejects the bare `Function` type; a precise construct signature is both\n// assignable to TypeORM's `Function` slot AND keeps the collected values\n// typed as classes rather than arbitrary callables.\ntype EntityClass = new (...args: never[]) => object;\n\n// Synchronous module loader bound to this spec's location. `import`/`await\n// import()` cannot be used here: loadMigrationClassesFromDir is invoked inside\n// `it.each(...)` arguments that Jest evaluates synchronously at collection\n// time, so the load must be synchronous. `createRequire(__filename)` resolves\n// migration classes and the cross-package schema-drift-validator through\n// ts-jest at runtime, so the static type-check graph never reaches across\n// project rootDirs — the same dynamic-load pattern the db-migrate\n// platform-bootstrap.integration.spec.ts relies on.\nconst requireModule = createRequire(__filename);\n\n// Structured logger for the (rare) entity-load skip path — `console.*` is\n// banned platform-wide; the NestJS Logger keeps the diagnostic on the same\n// structured-logging path the runtime services use.\nconst logger = new Logger('bootstrap-from-scratch');\n\n// Local narrowing helper. db-migrate has @typescript-eslint/no-non-null-assertion\n// ENABLED (unlike the e2e project, whose assertDefined SSoT this replaces), so a\n// bare `!` is banned here. assertDefined narrows `T | undefined` to `T` and fails\n// loudly at the assertion site instead of surfacing as a cryptic\n// \"cannot read properties of undefined\" further downstream.\nfunction assertDefined<T>(value: T | null | undefined, message?: string): T {\n  if (value === null || value === undefined) {\n    throw new Error(message ?? 'assertDefined: expected a value but received null/undefined');","numLines":120,"startLine":1,"totalLines":1416}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0191TDr7zws52xHSCqaabiKy","type":"tool_result","content":"200\t *     timestamped TypeORM migration files. Two patterns exist in this\n201\t *     codebase (historical drift, ADR-011 §\"Migration Runners\"):\n202\t *       * `apps/<svc>/src/migrations/`         (auth, admin-api, messaging, event-store)\n203\t *       * `apps/<svc>/src/database/migrations/` (everything else)\n204\t *     This manifest reflects the actual on-disk layout — keep in sync\n205\t *     when adding a service.\n206\t *\n207\t * Order matters: services with cross-schema FK migrations (e.g.\n208\t * messaging.tenant_ai_settings -> auth.tenants) must run after the\n209\t * schema they reference. We run auth FIRST for that reason; everything\n210\t * else is alphabetical.\n211\t */\n212\tinterface ServiceManifest {\n213\t  name: string;\n214\t  schema: string;\n215\t  migrationsDir: string;\n216\t  /**\n217\t   * `entitiesGlob`: optional glob (relative to repo root) that matches\n218\t   * every `*.entity.ts` file owned by this service. When set, the\n219\t   * entity-surface-vs-DB matrix (assertEntitySurfaceMatchesDb) iterates\n220\t   * the files under this glob and asserts each entity's table surface\n221\t   * exists in the DB. Services without entities (gateway-api) leave it\n222\t   * unset.\n223\t   */\n224\t  entitiesGlob?: string;\n225\t}\n226\t\n227\tconst SERVICES: ServiceManifest[] = [\n228\t  // auth FIRST — every other service's FKs reference auth.tenants.\n229\t  {\n230\t    name: 'auth-service',\n231\t    schema: 'auth',\n232\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/auth-service/src/migrations',\n233\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/auth-service/src',\n234\t  },\n235\t  // Then the rest, ordered to minimize cross-schema FK ordering hazards.\n236\t  {\n237\t    name: 'farm-service',\n238\t    schema: 'farm',\n239\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations',\n240\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/farm-service/src',\n241\t  },\n242\t  {\n243\t    name: 'sensor-service',\n244\t    schema: 'sensor',\n245\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations',\n246\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/sensor-service/src',\n247\t  },\n248\t  {\n249\t    name: 'hr-service',\n250\t    schema: 'hr',\n251\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations',\n252\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/hr-service/src',\n253\t  },\n254\t  {\n255\t    name: 'messaging-service',\n256\t    schema: 'messaging',\n257\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/messaging-service/src/migrations',\n258\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/messaging-service/src',\n259\t  },\n260\t  {\n261\t    name: 'alert-engine',\n262\t    schema: 'alert',\n263\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/alert-engine/src/database/migrations',\n264\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/alert-engine/src',\n265\t  },\n266\t  {\n267\t    name: 'billing-service',\n268\t    schema: 'billing',\n269\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/billing-service/src/database/migrations',\n270\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/billing-service/src',\n271\t  },\n272\t  {\n273\t    name: 'notification-service',\n274\t    schema: 'notification',\n275\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/notification-service/src/database/migrations',\n276\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/notification-service/src',\n277\t  },\n278\t  {\n279\t    name: 'observability-service',\n280\t    schema: 'observability',\n281\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/observability-service/src/database/migrations',\n282\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/observability-service/src',\n283\t  },\n284\t  {\n285\t    name: 'admin-api-service',\n286\t    schema: 'admin',\n287\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/admin-api-service/src/migrations',\n288\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/admin-api-service/src',\n289\t  },\n290\t  {\n291\t    name: 'event-store-service',\n292\t    schema: 'event_store',\n293\t    migrationsDir: 'apps/event-store-service/src/migrations',\n294\t    entitiesGlob: 'apps/event-store-service/src',\n295\t  },\n296\t  // Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — close the manifest gap. Two patterns exist in this\n *     codebase (historical drift, ADR-011 §\"Migration Runners\"):\n *       * `apps/<svc>/src/migrations/`         (auth, admin-api, messaging, event-store)\n *       * `apps/<svc>/src/database/migrations/` (everything else)\n *     This manifest reflects the actual on-disk layout — keep in sync\n *     when adding a service.\n *\n * Order matters: services with cross-schema FK migrations (e.g.\n * messaging.tenant_ai_settings -> auth.tenants) must run after the\n * schema they reference. We run auth FIRST for that reason; everything\n * else is alphabetical.\n */\ninterface ServiceManifest {\n  name: string;\n  schema: string;\n  migrationsDir: string;\n  /**\n   * `entitiesGlob`: optional glob (relative to repo root) that matches\n   * every `*.entity.ts` file owned by this service. When set, the\n   * entity-surface-vs-DB matrix (assertEntitySurfaceMatchesDb) iterates\n   * the files under this glob and asserts each entity's table surface\n   * exists in the DB. Services without entities (gateway-api) leave it\n   * unset.\n   */\n  entitiesGlob?: string;\n}\n\nconst SERVICES: ServiceManifest[] = [\n  // auth FIRST — every other service's FKs reference auth.tenants.\n  {\n    name: 'auth-service',\n    schema: 'auth',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/auth-service/src/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/auth-service/src',\n  },\n  // Then the rest, ordered to minimize cross-schema FK ordering hazards.\n  {\n    name: 'farm-service',\n    schema: 'farm',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/farm-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'sensor-service',\n    schema: 'sensor',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/sensor-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'hr-service',\n    schema: 'hr',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/hr-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n 'alert-engine',\n    schema: 'alert',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/alert-engine/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/alert-engine/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'billing-service',\n    schema: 'billing',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/billing-service/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/billing-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'notification-service',\n    schema: 'notification',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/notification-service/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/notification-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'observability-service',\n    schema: 'observability',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/observability-service/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/observability-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'admin-api-service',\n    schema: 'admin',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/admin-api-service/src/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/admin-api-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'event-store-service',\n    schema: 'event_store',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/event-store-service/src/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/event-store-service/src',\n  },\n  // Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — close the manifest gap. The 4 services\n  // below were absent from the original 11-service manifest, so the\n  // bootstrap test passed even when their migration chains were empty\n  // or their entity-surface drifted from the on-disk schema. Listed in the manifest so the schema-ownership\n  //     audit (Part B B.4) can assert the `gateway` schema exists\n  //     and is owned by `gateway_service`. `entitiesGlob` is unset\n  //     → entity-surface check skips it (correct: nothing to check).\n  {\n    name: 'hydroponics-service',\n    schema: 'hydroponics',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/hydroponics-service/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/hydroponics-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'ai-service',\n    schema: 'ai',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/ai-service/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/ai-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'config-service',\n    schema: 'config',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/config-service/src/database/migrations',\n    entitiesGlob: 'apps/config-service/src',\n  },\n  {\n    name: 'gateway-api',\n    schema: 'gateway',\n    migrationsDir: 'apps/gateway-api/src/database/migrations',\n    // gateway-api owns no entities — `entitiesGlob` intentionally unset.\n  },\n];\n\n/**\n * Tables expected to be registered as TimescaleDB hypertables in the SOURCE","numLines":140,"startLine":200,"totalLines":1416}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014vXZwPmmEYUKW9C2LaFRdM","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { readFileSync } from 'fs';\n2\timport { basename, join, relative } from 'path';\n3\timport { execFileSync } from 'child_process';\n4\t\n5\timport { MODULE_SCHEMAS } from '../../libs/backend-common/src/database/schema-manager.service';\n6\timport { stripComments } from './helpers/ts-source';\n7\t\n8\t/**\n9\t * Tenant provisioning is migration REPLAY: the orchestrator pins\n10\t * `search_path` to `tenant_<uuid>` and runs each tenant-aware service's\n11\t * migration set. That mechanism only redirects UNQUALIFIED DDL. Anything that\n12\t * names its schema explicitly — or re-pins the session to the source schema —\n13\t * escapes it, lands in the source schema, and is STILL recorded in the tenant's\n14\t * ledger as applied. This spec is the gate for that class.\n15\t *\n16\t * ## Why this spec was rewritten (DATA-HIGH-012)\n17\t *\n18\t * The previous detector was a single regex looking for a DDL keyword followed\n19\t * by a `\"<source schema>\".` identifier, plus a filename allowlist and a blanket\n20\t * exclusion for every Baseline. It enumerated one SPELLING of the defect rather\n21\t * than testing the property, and both halves of that leaked:\n22\t *\n23\t *   - MSG-HIGH-077 shipped a per-tenant migration that re-pinned its own source\n24\t *     schema — functionally identical to qualifying every statement in the file\n25\t *     — CITING THIS GUARD in its docblock as evidence of its own correctness.\n26\t *   - DATA-CRITICAL-010 (no new tenant can be provisioned) lived entirely\n27\t *     inside the Baseline exclusion.\n28\t *\n29\t * It now asks the actual question — *does this migration issue DDL against a\n30\t * PER-TENANT table in the source schema, by either spelling?* — using\n31\t * `MODULE_SCHEMAS` as the classifier. That is the same SSoT the entity layer\n32\t * obeys via `entity-schema-declaration`, so the guard cannot drift from it, and\n33\t * the Baselines are now checked like every other migration rather than excused.\n34\t *\n35\t * `scripts/migration/dequalify-tenant-baselines.mjs` reads the same registry to\n36\t * decide what to rewrite. Two consumers of one SSoT, not two SSoTs.\n37\t *\n38\t * ## Fail-closed\n39\t *\n40\t * A qualified identifier the registry cannot explain is a VIOLATION, not a\n41\t * pass. An unregistered per-tenant table is exactly the shape that would\n42\t * otherwise slip through, so \"I don't recognise this\" resolves against the\n43\t * migration and lands in the reviewer allowlist below with a written reason.\n44\t */\n45\t\n46\tconst REPO_ROOT = join(__dirname, '..', '..');\n47\t\n48\t/** Source schema per tenant-aware migration directory. */\n49\tconst TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string]> = [\n50\t  ['apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations', 'farm'],\n51\t  ['apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations', 'sensor'],\n52\t  ['apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations', 'hr'],\n53\t  ['apps/messaging-service/src/migrations', 'messaging'],\n54\t  ['apps/messaging-service/src/database/migrations', 'messaging'],\n55\t  ['apps/alert-engine/src/database/migrations', 'alert'],\n56\t  ['apps/ai-service/src/database/migrations', 'ai'],\n57\t  ['apps/hydroponics-service/src/database/migrations', 'hydroponics'],\n58\t];\n59\t\n60\t/** A DDL verb + object immediately preceding a qualified identifier. */\n61\tconst DDL_LEAD =\n62\t  /\\b(?:CREATE|ALTER|DROP|TRUNCATE)\\s+(?:UNIQUE\\s+)?(?:TABLE|INDEX|TYPE|SEQUENCE|VIEW|MATERIALIZED\\s+VIEW)\\b(?:\\s+IF\\s+(?:NOT\\s+)?EXISTS)?[\\s\\S]{0,220}$/i;\n63\t\n64\t/**\n65\t * Tables that exist in a shipped migration's DDL but in NEITHER\n66\t * `MODULE_SCHEMAS[].tables` nor `[].infrastructureTables`, because a later\n67\t * migration retired them. They cannot be classified and they cannot be edited\n68\t * out of an immutable migration, so they are named here with the migration that\n69\t * retired them. A NEW name appearing in a failure means an entity shipped\n70\t * without its registry entry — register it, do not add it here.\n71\t */\n72\tconst RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlySet<string>> = new Map([\n73\t  // Superseded by the `sensor_metrics` hypertable; the per-tenant copy of that\n74\t  // is created unqualified by 1815000000000.\n75\t  ['sensor', new Set(['sensor_readings'])],\n76\t  // Dropped by 1802100000000-DropTenantAiSettings.\n77\t  ['messaging', new Set(['tenant_ai_settings'])],\n78\t]);\n79\t\n80\t/**\n81\t * Migrations whose source-schema-qualified DDL has been REVIEWED as legitimate.\n82\t * This is a reviewer-gated allowlist in THIS spec, not a self-service docblock\n83\t * marker: ORPHAN-HIGH-408's `CreateAiProposedActions` used the old\n84\t * `TENANT_AWARE_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL_OK` docblock marker to schema-qualify a\n85\t * PER-TENANT table, which the replay then never landed in any tenant. A\n86\t * self-service marker bypasses CI silently; an allowlist edit shows up in the\n87\t * PR diff. Adding an entry must be justified in writing.\n88\t */\n89\tconst REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n90\t  // Source-only enum reclaim/heal of the 2026-07-07 QualityGrade outage.\n91\t  '1804400000000-DropOrphanQualityGradeEnum.ts',\n92\t  '1804500000000-HealBehindTenantQualityGrade.ts',\n93\t  // Cross-tenant source-schema tables + partition-contract index (not cloned).\n94\t  '1800300000000-SensorV2TenantFkAndLicenseGrant.ts',\n95\t  '1802000000000-AddVfdDeviceModelSeriesPumpTags.ts',\n96\t  '1804000000000-ConsolidateVfdRegisterMappingsToSensorSchema.ts',\n97\t  // SENSOR-CRITICAL-007 Slice 1: nullable edge-binding columns on the per-tenant\n98\t  // vfd_devices table via the SAME source-template + tenant fan-out pattern as\n99\t  // 1802000000000 above (canonical `sensor` copy + every `tenant_*` copy). Not a\n100\t  // per-tenant table wrongly schema-qualified — the fan-out lands it everywhere.\n101\t  '1808000000000-AddVfdDeviceEdgeBinding.ts',\n102\t  // SEC-MEDIUM-083: partial unique index on the per-tenant vfd_change_sets\n103\t  // table — canonical `sensor` copy + tenant fan-out (same pattern as\n104\t  // 1808000000000 above). Per-tenant, not misqualified.\n105\t  '1818000000000-EnforceSingleActiveVfdChangeSet.ts',\n106\t  // SENSOR-HIGH-064: nullable config-ack tracking columns on the per-tenant\n107\t  // edge_devices table via the SAME source-template + tenant fan-out pattern\n108\t  // (canonical `sensor` copy + every `tenant_*` copy).\n109\t  '1809000000000-AddEdgeDeviceConfigAckTracking.ts',\n110\t  // SENSOR-HIGH-083: per-tenant calibration_events table + nullable\n111\t  // calibration_interval_days column on sensor_data_channels, both created in the\n112\t  // canonical `sensor` source schema and fanned out into every `tenant_*` schema\n113\t  // (CREATE TABLE … LIKE INCLUDING ALL for the clone). Per-tenant, not misqualified.\n114\t  '1810000000000-AddCalibrationEventsAndInterval.ts',\n115\t  '1803000000000-HashProvisioningSecretsAtRest.ts',\n116\t  '1800500000000-EnsureMessagingPartitionContract.ts',\n117\t  // ORPHAN-HIGH-408: immutable historical BUG — `ai_proposed_actions` is a\n118\t  // per-tenant table wrongly schema-qualified. Retained only because migrations\n119\t  // are immutable; HEALED by 1803100000000-HealAiProposedActionsUnqualified.\n120\t  // DO NOT copy this pattern: per-tenant tables must be UNQUALIFIED.\n121\t  '1803000000000-CreateAiProposedActions.ts',\n122\t]);\n123\t\n124\tinterface Registry {\n125\t  readonly perTenant: ReadonlySet<string>;\n126\t  readonly infrastructure: ReadonlySet<string>;\n127\t  readonly retired: ReadonlySet<string>;\n128\t}\n129\t\n130\tfunction registryFor(schema: string): Registry {\n131\t  const entry = MODULE_SCHEMAS.find((m) => m.sourceSchema === schema);\n132\t  if (entry === undefined) {\n133\t    throw new Error(`MODULE_SCHEMAS has no entry for source schema \"${schema}\"`);\n134\t  }\n135\t  return {\n136\t    perTenant: new Set([...entry.tables, ...(entry.referenceDataTables ?? [])]),\n137\t    infrastructure: new Set(entry.infrastructureTables ?? []),\n138\t    retired: RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES.get(schema) ?? new Set<string>(),\n139\t  };\n140\t}\n141\t\n142\t/**\n143\t * `CREATE [UNIQUE] INDEX \"name\" ON \"schema\".\"table\"` — an index inherits its\n144\t * table's classification, and `DROP INDEX \"schema\".\"name\"` names no table, so\n145\t * the map has to be built from the create side first.\n146\t */\n147\tfunction indexOwners(source: string, schema: string): Map<string, string> {\n148\t  const owners = new Map<string, string>();\n149\t  const rx = new RegExp(\n150\t    `CREATE\\\\s+(?:UNIQUE\\\\s+)?INDEX\\\\s+(?:IF\\\\s+NOT\\\\s+EXISTS\\\\s+)?\"([^\"]+)\"\\\\s+ON\\\\s+(?:\"${schema}\"\\\\.)?\"([^\"]+)\"`,\n151\t    'gi',\n152\t  );\n153\t  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n154\t    if (match[1] !== undefined && match[2] !== undefined) owners.set(match[1], match[2]);\n155\t  }\n156\t  return owners;\n157\t}\n158\t\n159\t/**\n160\t * Enum type names from their own definitions rather than from a naming\n161\t * convention — farm's `equipment_category` is an enum and carries no `_enum`\n162\t * suffix. Enums are DELIBERATELY left qualified: existing tenants SHARE the\n163\t * source-schema type (`CREATE TABLE … LIKE INCLUDING ALL` shares a type, it does\n164\t * not clone it), so de-qualifying would split the fleet in two.\n165\t */\n166\tfunction enumTypes(source: string, schema: string): Set<string> {\n167\t  const names = new Set<string>();\n168\t  const rx = new RegExp(`CREATE\\\\s+TYPE\\\\s+\"${schema}\"\\\\.\"([^\"]+)\"\\\\s+AS\\\\s+ENUM`, 'gi');\n169\t  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n170\t    if (match[1] !== undefined) names.add(match[1]);\n171\t  }\n172\t  return names;\n173\t}\n174\t\n175\ttype Verdict = 'per-tenant' | 'cross-tenant' | 'enum' | 'retired' | 'unclassified';\n176\t\n177\tfunction classify(\n178\t  identifier: string,\n179\t  registry: Registry,\n180\t  indexes: Map<string, string>,\n181\t  enums: Set<string>,\n182\t): Verdict {\n183\t  if (enums.has(identifier)) return 'enum';\n184\t  if (registry.perTenant.has(identifier)) return 'per-tenant';\n185\t  if (registry.infrastructure.has(identifier)) return 'cross-tenant';\n186\t  if (registry.retired.has(identifier)) return 'retired';\n187\t  const owner = indexes.get(identifier);\n188\t  if (owner !== undefined && owner !== identifier) {\n189\t    return classify(owner, registry, indexes, enums);\n190\t  }\n191\t  return 'unclassified';\n192\t}\n193\t\n194\tinterface Offence {\n195\t  readonly file: string;\n196\t  readonly reason: string;\n197\t}\n198\t\n199\t/** Qualified DDL naming something that must not live in the source schema. */\n200\tfunction qualifiedDdlOffences(file: string, source: string, schema: string): Offence[] {\n201\t  const indexes = indexOwners(source, schema);\n202\t  const enums = enumTypes(source, schema);\n203\t  const registry = registryFor(schema);\n204\t  const offences: Offence[] = [];\n205\t  const seen = new Set<string>();\n206\t\n207\t  const rx = new RegExp(`\"${schema}\"\\\\.\"([^\"]+)\"`, 'g');\n208\t  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n209\t    const identifier = match[1];\n210\t    if (identifier === undefined) continue;\n211\t    // Only DDL counts. A qualified name inside a DML statement, a\n212\t    // `to_regclass()` probe or a RAISE message is not a routing decision.\n213\t    if (!DDL_LEAD.test(source.slice(0, match.index))) continue;\n214\t\n215\t    const verdict = classify(identifier, registry, indexes, enums);\n216\t    if (verdict === 'cross-tenant' || verdict === 'enum' || verdict === 'retired') continue;\n217\t    if (seen.has(identifier)) continue;\n218\t    seen.add(identifier);\n219\t\n220\t    offences.push({\n221\t      file,\n222\t      reason:\n223\t        verdict === 'per-tenant'\n224\t          ? `DDL on \"${schema}\".\"${identifier}\" — a PER-TENANT table (MODULE_SCHEMAS.${schema}.tables). ` +\n225\t            `The replay's search_path cannot redirect a qualified name, so every tenant pass writes to ` +\n226\t            `the source schema and the tenant ledger still records it as applied. Drop the \"${schema}\". prefix.`\n227\t          : `DDL on \"${schema}\".\"${identifier}\" — not in MODULE_SCHEMAS.${schema}.tables OR ` +\n228\t            `.infrastructureTables, so this guard cannot tell whether it belongs in a tenant schema. ` +\n229\t            `Register the table, or add the migration to REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL with a written reason.`,\n230\t    });\n231\t  }\n232\t  return offences;\n233\t}\n234\t\n235\t/**\n236\t * `pinSearchPath(qr, '<own source schema>')` in a migration that is NOT\n237\t * `@SourceOnlyMigration` — the second spelling of the same defect. The helper\n238\t * (`libs/backend-common/src/database/base-migration.ts`) issues\n239\t * `SET search_path TO \"<its literal argument>\", public` and has no tenant\n240\t * awareness, so it OVERRIDES the runner's tenant pin for the rest of the\n241\t * migration. Legitimate on a source-only migration, where the tenant pass skips\n242\t * the file entirely; a silent tenant-data hole anywhere else.\n243\t */\n244\tfunction sourcePinOffences(file: string, source: string, schema: string): Offence[] {\n245\t  if (/@SourceOnlyMigration\\s*\\(/.test(source)) return [];\n246\t  const rx = new RegExp(\n247\t    `\\\\bpinSearchPath\\\\s*\\\\(\\\\s*[A-Za-z_$][\\\\w$]*\\\\s*,\\\\s*['\"\\`]${schema}['\"\\`]`,\n248\t    'g',\n249\t  );\n250\t  if (!rx.test(source)) return [];\n251\t  return [\n252\t    {\n253\t      file,\n254\t      reason:\n255\t        `pinSearchPath(queryRunner, '${schema}') in a migration that is not @SourceOnlyMigration. ` +\n256\t        `It pins its literal argument, so a tenant pass re-pins the SOURCE schema, the DDL no-ops ` +\n257\t        `against rows that already exist there, and the tenant ledger records a successful apply ` +\n258\t        `anyway (MSG-HIGH-077). Either drop the pin and follow the runner's, or mark the migration ` +\n259\t        `@SourceOnlyMigration if the table really is cross-tenant.`,\n260\t    },\n261\t  ];\n262\t}\n263\t\n264\tfunction args, { cwd: REPO_ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' })\n267\t    .split('\\n')\n268\t    .map((line) => line.trim())\n269\t    .filter(Boolean);\n270\t\n271\t  return files.map((file) => {\n272\t    const match const offences: Offence[] = [];\n292\t    for (const [file, schema] of migrationFiles()) {\n293\t      const source = stripComments(readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8'));\n294\t      offences.push(...sourcePinOffences(relative(REPO_ROOT, file), source, schema));\n295\t    }\n296\t\n297\t    expect(offences.map((o) => `${o.file}: ${o.reason}`)).toEqual([]);\n298\t [...REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL].filter((name) => !present.has(name));\n303\t    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n304\t  });\n305\t\n306\t  it('every retired-table entry names a table the registry really does not carry', () => {\n307\t    const stale: string[] = [];\n308\t    for (const [schema, tables] of RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES) {\n309\t      const registry = registryFor(schema);\n310\t      for
