{ readFileSync } from 'fs';\nimport { basename, join, relative } from 'path';\nimport { execFileSync } from 'child_process';\n\nimport { MODULE_SCHEMAS } from '../../libs/backend-common/src/database/schema-manager.service';\nimport { stripComments } from './helpers/ts-source';\n\n/**\n * Tenant provisioning is migration REPLAY: the orchestrator pins\n * `search_path` to `tenant_<uuid>` and runs each tenant-aware service's\n * migration set. That mechanism only redirects UNQUALIFIED DDL. Anything that\n * names its schema explicitly — or re-pins the session to the source schema —\n * escapes it, lands in the source schema, and is STILL recorded in the tenant's\n * ledger as applied. This spec is the gate for that class.\n *\n * ## Why this spec was rewritten (DATA-HIGH-012)\n *\n * The previous detector was a single regex looking for a DDL keyword followed\n * by a `\"<source schema>\".` identifier, plus a filename allowlist and a blanket\n * exclusion for every Baseline. It enumerated one SPELLING of the defect rather\n * than testing the property, and both halves of that leaked:\n *\n *   - MSG-HIGH-077 shipped a per-tenant migration that re-pinned its own source\n *     schema — functionally identical to qualifying every statement in the file\n *     — CITING THIS GUARD in its docblock as evidence of its own correctness.\n *   - DATA-CRITICAL-010 (no new tenant can be provisioned) lived entirely\n *     inside the Baseline exclusion.\n *\n * It now asks the actual question — *does this migration issue DDL against a\n * PER-TENANT table in the source schema, by either spelling?* — using\n * `MODULE_SCHEMAS` as the classifier. That is the same SSoT the entity layer\n * obeys via `entity-schema-declaration`, so the guard cannot drift from it, and\n * the Baselines are now checked like every other migration rather than excused.\n *\n * `scripts/migration/dequalify-tenant-baselines.mjs` reads the same registry to\n * decide what to rewrite. Two consumers of one SSoT, not two SSoTs.\n *\n * ## Fail-closed\n *\n * A qualified identifier the registry cannot explain is a VIOLATION, not a\n * pass. An unregistered per-tenant table is exactly the shape that would\n * otherwise slip through, so \"I don't recognise this\" resolves against the\n * migration and lands in the reviewer allowlist below with a written reason.\n */\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = join(__dirname, '..', '..');\n\n/** Source schema per tenant-aware migration directory. */\nconst TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [string, string]> = [\n  ['apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations', 'farm'],\n  ['apps/sensor-service/src/database/migrations', 'sensor'],\n  ['apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations', 'hr'],\n  ['apps/messaging-service/src/migrations', 'messaging'],\n  ['apps/messaging-service/src/database/migrations', 'messaging'],\n  ['apps/alert-engine/src/database/migrations', 'alert'],\n  ['apps/ai-service/src/database/migrations', 'ai'],\n  ['apps/hydroponics-service/src/database/migrations', 'hydroponics'],\n];\n\n/** A DDL verb + object immediately preceding a qualified identifier. */\nconst DDL_LEAD =\n  /\\b(?:CREATE|ALTER|DROP|TRUNCATE)\\s+(?:UNIQUE\\s+)?(?:TABLE|INDEX|TYPE|SEQUENCE|VIEW|MATERIALIZED\\s+VIEW)\\b(?:\\s+IF\\s+(?:NOT\\s+)?EXISTS)?[\\s\\S]{0,220}$/i;\n\n/**\n * Tables that exist in a shipped migration's DDL but in NEITHER\n * `MODULE_SCHEMAS[].tables` nor `[].infrastructureTables`, because a later\n * migration retired them. They cannot be classified and they cannot be edited\n * out of an immutable migration, so they are named here with the migration that\n * retired them. A NEW name appearing in a failure means an entity shipped\n * without its registry entry — register it, do not add it here.\n */\nconst RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlySet<string>> = new Map([\n  // Superseded by the `sensor_metrics` hypertable; the per-tenant copy of that\n  // is created unqualified by 1815000000000.\n  ['sensor', new Set(['sensor_readings'])],\n  // Dropped by 1802100000000-DropTenantAiSettings.\n  ['messaging', new Set(['tenant_ai_settings'])],\n]);\n\n/**\n * Migrations whose source-schema-qualified DDL has been REVIEWED as legitimate.\n * This is a reviewer-gated allowlist in THIS spec, not a self-service docblock\n * marker: ORPHAN-HIGH-408's `CreateAiProposedActions` used the old\n * `TENANT_AWARE_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL_OK` docblock marker to schema-qualify a\n * PER-TENANT table, which the replay then never landed in any tenant. A\n * self-service marker bypasses CI silently; an allowlist edit shows up in the\n * PR diff. Adding an entry must be justified in writing.\n */\nconst REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n  // Source-only enum reclaim/heal of the 2026-07-07 QualityGrade outage.\n  '1804400000000-DropOrphanQualityGradeEnum.ts',\n  '1804500000000-HealBehindTenantQualityGrade.ts',\n  // Cross-tenant source-schema tables + partition-contract index (not cloned).\n  '1800300000000-SensorV2TenantFkAndLicenseGrant.ts',\n  '1802000000000-AddVfdDeviceModelSeriesPumpTags.ts',\n  '1804000000000-ConsolidateVfdRegisterMappingsToSensorSchema.ts',\n  // SENSOR-CRITICAL-007 Slice 1: nullable edge-binding columns on the per-tenant\n  // vfd_devices table via the SAME source-template + tenant fan-out pattern as\n  // 1802000000000 above (canonical `sensor` copy + every `tenant_*` copy). Not a\n  // per-tenant table wrongly schema-qualified — the fan-out lands it everywhere.\n  '1808000000000-AddVfdDeviceEdgeBinding.ts',\n  // SEC-MEDIUM-083: partial unique index on the per-tenant vfd_change_sets\n  // table — canonical `sensor` copy + tenant fan-out (same pattern as\n  // 1808000000000 above). Per-tenant, not misqualified.\n  '1818000000000-EnforceSingleActiveVfdChangeSet.ts',\n  // SENSOR-HIGH-064: nullable config-ack tracking columns on the per-tenant\n  // edge_devices table via the SAME source-template + tenant fan-out pattern\n  // (canonical `sensor` copy + every `tenant_*` copy).\n  '1809000000000-AddEdgeDeviceConfigAckTracking.ts',\n  // SENSOR-HIGH-083: per-tenant calibration_events table + nullable\n  // calibration_interval_days column on sensor_data_channels, both created in the\n  // canonical `sensor` source schema and fanned out into every `tenant_*` schema\n  // (CREATE TABLE … LIKE INCLUDING ALL for the clone). Per-tenant, not misqualified.\n  '1810000000000-AddCalibrationEventsAndInterval.ts',\n  '1803000000000-HashProvisioningSecretsAtRest.ts',\n  '1800500000000-EnsureMessagingPartitionContract.ts',\n  // ORPHAN-HIGH-408: immutable historical BUG — `ai_proposed_actions` is a\n  // per-tenant table wrongly schema-qualified. Retained only because migrations\n  // are immutable; HEALED by 1803100000000-HealAiProposedActionsUnqualified.\n  // DO NOT copy this pattern: per-tenant tables must be UNQUALIFIED.\n  '1803000000000-CreateAiProposedActions.ts',\n]);\n\ninterface Registry {\n  readonly perTenant: ReadonlySet<string>;\n  readonly infrastructure: ReadonlySet<string>;\n  readonly retired: ReadonlySet<string>;\n}\n\nfunction registryFor(schema: string): Registry {\n  const entry = MODULE_SCHEMAS.find((m) => m.sourceSchema === schema);\n  if (entry === undefined) {\n    throw new Error(`MODULE_SCHEMAS has no entry for source schema \"${schema}\"`);\n  }\n  return {\n    perTenant: new Set([...entry.tables, ...(entry.referenceDataTables ?? [])]),\n    infrastructure: new Set(entry.infrastructureTables ?? []),\n    retired: RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES.get(schema) ?? new Set<string>(),\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * `CREATE [UNIQUE] INDEX \"name\" ON \"schema\".\"table\"` — an index inherits its\n * table's classification, and `DROP INDEX \"schema\".\"name\"` names no table, so\n * the map has to be built from the create side first.\n */\nfunction indexOwners(source: string, schema: string): Map<string, string> {\n  const owners = new Map<string, string>();\n  const rx = new RegExp(\n    `CREATE\\\\s+(?:UNIQUE\\\\s+)?INDEX\\\\s+(?:IF\\\\s+NOT\\\\s+EXISTS\\\\s+)?\"([^\"]+)\"\\\\s+ON\\\\s+(?:\"${schema}\"\\\\.)?\"([^\"]+)\"`,\n    'gi',\n  );\n  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n    if (match[1] !== undefined && match[2] !== undefined) owners.set(match[1], match[2]);\n  }\n  return owners;\n}\n\n/**\n * Enum type names from their own definitions rather than from a naming\n * convention — farm's `equipment_category` is an enum and carries no `_enum`\n * suffix. Enums are DELIBERATELY left qualified: existing tenants SHARE the\n * source-schema type (`CREATE TABLE … LIKE INCLUDING ALL` shares a type, it does\n * not clone it), so de-qualifying would split the fleet in two.\n */\nfunction enumTypes(source: string, schema: string): Set<string> {\n  const names = new Set<string>();\n  const rx = new RegExp(`CREATE\\\\s+TYPE\\\\s+\"${schema}\"\\\\.\"([^\"]+)\"\\\\s+AS\\\\s+ENUM`, 'gi');\n  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n    if (match[1] !== undefined) names.add(match[1]);\n  }\n  return names;\n}\n\ntype Verdict = 'per-tenant' | 'cross-tenant' | 'enum' | 'retired' | 'unclassified';\n\nfunction classify(\n  identifier: string,\n  registry: Registry,\n  indexes: Map<string, string>,\n  enums: Set<string>,\n): Verdict {\n  if (enums.has(identifier)) return 'enum';\n  if (registry.perTenant.has(identifier)) return 'per-tenant';\n  if (registry.infrastructure.has(identifier)) return 'cross-tenant';\n  if (registry.retired.has(identifier)) return 'retired';\n  const owner = indexes.get(identifier);\n  if (owner !== undefined && owner !== identifier) {\n    return classify(owner, registry, indexes, enums);\n  }\n  return 'unclassified';\n}\n\ninterface Offence {\n  readonly file: string;\n  readonly reason: string;\n}\n\n/** Qualified DDL naming something that must not live in the source schema. */\nfunction qualifiedDdlOffences(file: string, source: string, schema: string): Offence[] {\n  const indexes = indexOwners(source, schema);\n  const enums = enumTypes(source, schema);\n  const registry = registryFor(schema);\n  const offences: Offence[] = [];\n  const seen = new Set<string>();\n\n  const rx = new RegExp(`\"${schema}\"\\\\.\"([^\"]+)\"`, 'g');\n  for (const match of source.matchAll(rx)) {\n    const identifier = match[1];\n    if (identifier === undefined) continue;\n    // Only DDL counts. A qualified name inside a DML statement, a\n    // `to_regclass()` probe or a RAISE message is not a routing decision.\n    if (!DDL_LEAD.test(source.slice(0, match.index))) continue;\n\n    const verdict = classify(identifier, registry, indexes, enums);\n    if (verdict === 'cross-tenant' || verdict === 'enum' || verdict === 'retired') continue;\n    if (seen.has(identifier)) continue;\n    seen.add(identifier);\n\n    offences.push({\n      file,\n      reason:\n        verdict === 'per-tenant'\n          ? `DDL on \"${schema}\".\"${identifier}\" — a PER-TENANT table (MODULE_SCHEMAS.${schema}.tables). ` +\n            `The replay's search_path cannot redirect a qualified name, so every tenant pass writes to ` +\n            `the source schema and the tenant ledger still records it as applied. Drop the \"${schema}\". prefix.`\n          : `DDL on \"${schema}\".\"${identifier}\" — not in MODULE_SCHEMAS.${schema}.tables OR ` +\n            `.infrastructureTables, so this guard cannot tell whether it belongs in a tenant schema. ` +\n            `Register the table, or add the migration to REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL with a written reason.`,\n    });\n  }\n  return offences;\n}\n\n/**\n * `pinSearchPath(qr, '<own source schema>')` in a migration that is NOT\n * `@SourceOnlyMigration` — the second spelling of the same defect. The helper\n * (`libs/backend-common/src/database/base-migration.ts`) issues\n * `SET search_path TO \"<its literal argument>\", public` and has no tenant\n * awareness, so it OVERRIDES the runner's tenant pin for the rest of the\n * migration. Legitimate on a source-only migration, where the tenant pass skips\n * the file entirely; a silent tenant-data hole anywhere else.\n */\nfunction sourcePinOffences(file: string, source: string, schema: string): Offence[] {\n  if (/@SourceOnlyMigration\\s*\\(/.test(source)) return [];\n  const rx = new RegExp(\n    `\\\\bpinSearchPath\\\\s*\\\\(\\\\s*[A-Za-z_$][\\\\w$]*\\\\s*,\\\\s*['\"\\`]${schema}['\"\\`]`,\n    'g',\n  );\n  if (!rx.test(source)) return [];\n  return [\n    {\n      file,\n      reason:\n        `pinSearchPath(queryRunner, '${schema}') in a migration that is not @SourceOnlyMigration. ` +\n        `It pins its literal argument, so a tenant pass re-pins the SOURCE schema, the DDL no-ops ` +\n        `against rows that already exist there, and the tenant ledger records a successful apply ` +\n        `anyway (MSG-HIGH-077). Either drop the pin and follow the runner's, or mark the migration ` +\n        `@SourceOnlyMigration if the table really is cross-tenant.`,\n    },\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction migrationFiles(): Array<readonly [string, string]> {\n  const args = ['ls-files', ...TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS.map(([dir]) => `${dir}/[0-9]*.ts`)];\n  const files = execFileSync('git', args, { cwd: REPO_ROOT, encoding: 'utf8' })\n    .split('\\n')\n    .map((line) => line.trim())\n    .filter(Boolean);\n\n  return files.map((file) => {\n    const match = TENANT_AWARE_MIGRATION_DIRS.find(([dir]) => file.startsWith(`${dir}/`));\n    if (match === undefined) throw new Error(`no source schema mapped for ${file}`);\n    return [file, match[1]] as const;\n  });\n}\n\ndescribe('tenant-aware migration DDL guard', () => {\n  it('no tenant-aware migration issues source-schema DDL against a per-tenant table', () => {\n    const offences: Offence[] = [];\n    for (const [file, schema] of migrationFiles()) {\n      if (REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL.has(basename(file))) continue;\n      const source = stripComments(readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8'));\n      offences.push(...qualifiedDdlOffences(relative(REPO_ROOT, file), source, schema));\n    }\n\n    expect(offences.map((o) => `${o.file}: ${o.reason}`)).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('no tenant-aware migration re-pins its own source schema unless it is source-only', () => {\n    const offences: Offence[] = [];\n    for (const [file, schema] of migrationFiles()) {\n      const source = stripComments(readFileSync(join(REPO_ROOT, file), 'utf8'));\n      offences.push(...sourcePinOffences(relative(REPO_ROOT, file), source, schema));\n    }\n\n    expect(offences.map((o) => `${o.file}: ${o.reason}`)).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every allowlisted migration still exists (no stale exemptions)', () => {\n    const present = new Set(migrationFiles().map(([f]) => basename(f)));\n    const stale = [...REVIEWED_SOURCE_SCHEMA_DDL].filter((name) => !present.has(name));\n    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  it('every retired-table entry names a table the registry really does not carry', () => {\n    const stale: string[] = [];\n    for (const [schema, tables] of RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES) {\n      const registry = registryFor(schema);\n      for (const table of tables) {\n        if (registry.perTenant.has(table) || registry.infrastructure.has(table)) {\n          stale.push(`${schema}.${table} is registered again — drop it from RETIRED_SOURCE_TABLES`);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    expect(stale).toEqual([]);\n  });\n});\n","numLines":319,"startLine":1,"totalLines":319}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Srvj6EnBepNRJaJ8x5TdFW","type":"tool_result","content":"440\t *\n441\t * Strategy: iterate the directory, filter to `<13-digit>-<Name>.ts` files\n442\t * (the codebase's enforced filename convention — see\n443\t * .github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml step \"Validate migration\n444\t * filename...\"), require each, and pull the default-or-named export\n445\t * whose name ends with the timestamp. Migration classes follow the\n446\t * convention `<Name><timestamp>` (e.g. `CreateInitialSchema1700000000000`).\n447\t */\n448\tfunction loadMigrationClassesFromDir(absDir: string): Array<new () => MigrationInterface> {\n449\t  // Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — services that own a directory but ship no\n450\t  // migrations yet (config-service: .gitkeep only; gateway-api:\n451\t  // directory absent entirely) are valid manifest entries; return an\n452\t  // empty class list so the per-service runMigrations() call is a no-op\n453\t  // rather than a hard failure.\n454\t  if (!existsSync(absDir)) {\n455\t    return [];\n456\t  }\n457\t\n458\t  let entries: string[];\n459\t  try {\n460\t    entries = readdirSync(absDir);\n461\t  } catch (err) {\n462\t    throw new Error(\n463\t      `Migration directory ${absDir} could not be read: ${(err as Error).message}. ` +\n464\t        `Update SERVICES manifest if the path moved.`,\n465\t    );\n466\t  }\n467\t\n468\t  const migrationFiles = entries\n469\t    .filter((f) => /^[0-9]{13}-[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*\\.ts$/.test(f))\n470\t    .filter((f) => !f.endsWith('.spec.ts') && !f.endsWith('.test.ts'))\n471\t    .sort(); // lexical sort = chronological because timestamps are zero-padded.\n472\t\n473\t  const classes: Array<new () => MigrationInterface> = [];\n474\t  for (const file of migrationFiles) {\n475\t    const fullPath = join(absDir, file);\n476\t    if (!statSync(fullPath).isFile()) continue;\n477\t\n478\t    // ts-jest transforms .ts files when loaded through requireModule here\n479\t    // because this spec runs under the ts-jest transform configured in\n480\t    // e2e/jest.config.ts. Each migration's `export class <Name>` becomes\n481\t    // a property on the loaded module object.\n482\t    const mod = requireModule(fullPath) as Record<string, unknown>;\n483\t    const candidate = Object.values(mod).find(\n484\t      (v): v is new () => MigrationInterface =>\n485\t        typeof v === 'function' && /^[A-Z]/.test(v.name) && /\\d{13}$/.test(v.name),\n486\t    );\n487\t    if (!candidate) {\n488\t      throw new Error(\n489\t        `Migration file ${fullPath} did not export a class whose name matches ` +\n490\t          `<PascalCaseName><13-digit-timestamp>. Filename + class-name convention ` +\n491\t          `is enforced by .github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml.`,\n492\t      );\n493\t    }\n494\t    classes.push(candidate);\n495\t  }\n496\t  return classes;\n497\t}\n498\t\n499\t/**\n500\t * Discover every `*.entity.ts` file under a service's source root.\n501\t *\n502\t * Used by the entity-surface coverage matrix (assertEntitySurfaceMatchesDb).\n503\t * Recurses through subdirectories because services scatter entities across\n504\t * `src/<feature>/entities/`, `src/<feature>/`, and the legacy\n505\t * `src/setup/entities/` location.\n506\t */\n507\tfunction findEntityFiles(absRoot: string): string[] {\n508\t  const out: string[] = [];\n509\t  if (!existsSync(absRoot)) return out;\n510\t  const stack: string[] = [absRoot];\n511\t  while (stack.length > 0) {\n512\t    // The `while (stack.length > 0)` guard makes `pop()` defined here;\n513\t    // assertDefined narrows it without a banned non-null assertion.\n514\t    const current = assertDefined(stack.pop());\n515\t    let entries: string[];\n516\t    try {\n517\t      entries = readdirSync(current);\n518\t    } catch {\n519\t      continue;"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d5296f0-6d3c-4867-b93d-0a25583fcc68","uuid":"0612ba36-185d-48a4-a313-6cf49a3958ea","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:36:18.771Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-0db550b56f62/apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts","content":" *\n * Strategy: iterate the directory, filter to `<13-digit>-<Name>.ts` files\n * (the codebase's enforced filename convention — see\n * .github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml step \"Validate migration\n * filename...\"), require each, and pull the default-or-named export\n * whose name ends with the timestamp. Migration classes follow the\n * convention `<Name><timestamp>` (e.g. `CreateInitialSchema1700000000000`).\n */\nfunction loadMigrationClassesFromDir(absDir: string): Array<new () => MigrationInterface> {\n  // Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — services that own a directory but ship no\n  // migrations yet (config-service: .gitkeep only; gateway-api:\n  // directory absent entirely) are valid manifest entries; return an\n  // empty class list so the per-service runMigrations() call is a no-op\n  // rather than a hard failure.\n  if (!existsSync(absDir)) {\n    return [];\n  }\n\n  let entries: string[];\n  try {\n    entries = readdirSync(absDir);\n  } catch (err) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Migration directory ${absDir} could not be read: ${(err as Error).message}. ` +\n        `Update SERVICES manifest if the path moved.`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  const migrationFiles = entries\n    .filter((f) => /^[0-9]{13}-[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*\\.ts$/.test(f))\n    .filter((f) => !f.endsWith('.spec.ts') && !f.endsWith('.test.ts'))\n    .sort(); // lexical sort = chronological because timestamps are zero-padded.\n\n  const classes: Array<new () => MigrationInterface> = [];\n  for (const file of migrationFiles) {\n    const fullPath = join(absDir, file);\n    if (!statSync(fullPath).isFile()) continue;\n\n    // ts-jest transforms .ts files when loaded through requireModule here\n    // because this spec runs under the ts-jest transform configured in\n    // e2e/jest.config.ts. Each migration's `export class <Name>` becomes\n    // a property on the loaded module object.\n    const mod = requireModule(fullPath) as Record<string, unknown>;\n    const candidate = Object.values(mod).find(\n      (v): v is new () => MigrationInterface =>\n        typeof v === 'function' && /^[A-Z]/.test(v.name) && /\\d{13}$/.test(v.name),\n    );\n    if (!candidate) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `Migration file ${fullPath} did not export a class whose name matches ` +\n          `<PascalCaseName><13-digit-timestamp>. Filename + class-name convention ` +\n          `is enforced by .github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml.`,\n      );\n    }\n    classes.push(candidate);\n  }\n  return classes;\n}\n\n/**\n * Discover every `*.entity.ts` file under a service's source root.\n *\n * Used by the entity-surface coverage matrix (assertEntitySurfaceMatchesDb).\n * Recurses through subdirectories because services scatter entities across\n * `src/<feature>/entities/`, `src/<feature>/`, and the legacy\n * `src/setup/entities/` location.\n */\nfunction findEntityFiles(absRoot: string): string[] {\n  const out: string[] = [];\n  if (!existsSync(absRoot)) return out;\n  const stack: string[] = [absRoot];\n  while (stack.length > 0) {\n    // The `while (stack.length > 0)` guard makes `pop()` defined here;\n    // assertDefined narrows it without a banned non-null assertion.\n    const current = assertDefined(stack.pop());\n    let entries: string[];\n    try {\n      entries = readdirSync(current);\n    } catch {\n      continue;","numLines":80,"startLine":440,"totalLines":1416}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DwWtFMdkXyN77sQbfVwnkN","type":"tool_result","content":"855\t      lockTimeoutSeconds: 60,\n856\t    });\n857\t\n858\t    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n859\t    // 2. Run every service's migration chain in dependency order.\n860\t    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n861\t    for (const svc of SERVICES) {\n862\t      const absDir = join(REPO_ROOT, svc.migrationsDir);\n863\t      const migrationClasses = loadMigrationClassesFromDir(absDir);\n864\t\n865\t      // Load entity classes for services that declare an entitiesGlob.\n866\t      // Required for migrations that introspect connection.entityMetadatas\n867\t      // — notably hr-service's SyncHrEntitiesToDb1786800000000 which\n868\t      // derives its DDL emit + entity-default registry from the entity\n869\t      // surface. Other services tolerate the empty-entities path (their\n870\t      // migrations are pure DDL). Services without entitiesGlob\n871\t      // (gateway-api) keep an empty list. Pairs with the\n872\t      // ALTER-COLUMN-TYPE default-recovery transform inside\n873\t      // SyncHrEntitiesToDb that prevents the \"default for column ...\n874\t      // cannot be cast automatically to type ..._enum\" failure mode on\n875\t      // fresh-volume bootstraps.\n876\t      const entityClasses: EntityClass[] = svc.entitiesGlob\n877\t        ? loadEntityClasses(join(REPO_ROOT, svc.entitiesGlob))\n878\t        : [];\n879\t\n880\t      const ds = new DataSource({\n881\t        type: 'postgres',\n882\t        host,\n883\t        port,\n884\t        username: DATABASE_USER,\n885\t        password: DATABASE_PASSWORD,\n886\t        database: DATABASE_NAME,\n887\t        schema: svc.schema,\n888\t        migrations: migrationClasses,\n889\t        entities: entityClasses,\n890\t        synchronize: false,\n891\t        migrationsRun: false,\n892\t        // Migration table inside the service schema; the production\n893\t        // MigrationRunnerService uses the same convention.\n894\t        migrationsTableName: 'migrations',\n895\t        // Pin search_path on every connection acquired from this pool.\n896\t        // Why: TypeORM's built-in MigrationExecutor (invoked by\n897\t        // ds.runMigrations() below) does NOT pin search_path —\n898\t        // production MigrationRunnerService does, but this test bypasses\n899\t        // the runner and goes straight to the executor. Migrations that\n900\t        // use unqualified table references (e.g. `ALTER TABLE \"users\"`,\n901\t        // `FROM \"users\"`) then resolve against the connection's default\n902\t        // search_path (`public`) and fail with `relation \"users\" does\n903\t        // not exist` on a fresh DB where auth tables only live in the\n904\t        // auth schema. The libpq `-c search_path=...` option sets the\n905\t        // path at session-start time, before any migration SQL runs,\n906\t        // and survives across the BEGIN/COMMIT cycles MigrationExecutor\n907\t        // uses in `transaction: 'each'` mode.\n908\t        extra: {\n909\t          options: `-c search_path=\"${svc.schema}\",public`,\n910\t        },\n911\t      });\n912\t\n913\t      try {\n914\t        await ds.initialize();\n915\t        // Skip runMigrations() for services with empty migration sets —\n916\t        // gateway-api owns no migrations dir and config-service ships\n917\t        // only `.gitkeep`. Calling runMigrations() with an empty list is\n918\t        // a no-op in TypeORM, but skipping the call avoids creating the\n919\t        // empty `<schema>.migrations` ledger table that would skew the\n920\t        // \"ledger is non-empty\" assertion below.\n921\t        if (migrationClasses.length > 0) {\n922\t          await runWithDbMigrateDdlAuthority(() => ds.runMigrations({ transaction: 'each' }));\n923\t        }\n924\t      } catch (err) {\n925\t        // Surface which service failed so the diagnostic message is\n926\t        // actionable from CI logs. Do not swallow — re-throw so Jest\n927\t        // marks the suite as failed.\n928\t        throw new Error(\n929\t          `[bootstrap-from-scratch] Migration run failed for service \"${svc.name}\" ` +"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d5296f0-6d3c-4867-b93d-0a25583fcc68","uuid":"937855f8-2caa-4aad-aa89-955335c040ba","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:36:19.938Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-0db550b56f62/apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts","content":"      lockTimeoutSeconds: 60,\n    });\n\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    // 2. Run every service's migration chain in dependency order.\n    // -----------------------------------------------------------------\n    for (const svc of SERVICES) {\n      const absDir = join(REPO_ROOT, svc.migrationsDir);\n      const migrationClasses = loadMigrationClassesFromDir(absDir);\n\n      // Load entity classes for services that declare an entitiesGlob.\n      // Required for migrations that introspect connection.entityMetadatas\n      // — notably hr-service's SyncHrEntitiesToDb1786800000000 which\n      // derives its DDL emit + entity-default registry from the entity\n      // surface. Other services tolerate the empty-entities path (their\n      // migrations are pure DDL). Services without entitiesGlob\n      // (gateway-api) keep an empty list. Pairs with the\n      // ALTER-COLUMN-TYPE default-recovery transform inside\n      // SyncHrEntitiesToDb that prevents the \"default for column ...\n      // cannot be cast automatically to type ..._enum\" failure mode on\n      // fresh-volume bootstraps.\n      const entityClasses: EntityClass[] = svc.entitiesGlob\n        ? loadEntityClasses(join(REPO_ROOT, svc.entitiesGlob))\n        : [];\n\n      const ds = new DataSource({\n        type: 'postgres',\n        host,\n        port,\n        username: DATABASE_USER,\n        password: DATABASE_PASSWORD,\n        database: DATABASE_NAME,\n        schema: svc.schema,\n        migrations: migrationClasses,\n        entities: entityClasses,\n        synchronize: false,\n        migrationsRun: false,\n        // Migration table inside the service schema; the production\n        // MigrationRunnerService uses the same convention.\n        migrationsTableName: 'migrations',\n        // Pin search_path on every connection acquired from this pool.\n        // Why: TypeORM's built-in MigrationExecutor (invoked by\n        // ds.runMigrations() below) does NOT pin search_path —\n        // production MigrationRunnerService does, but this test bypasses\n        // the runner and goes straight to the executor. Migrations that\n        // use unqualified table references (e.g. `ALTER TABLE \"users\"`,\n        // `FROM \"users\"`) then resolve against the connection's default\n        // search_path (`public`) and fail with `relation \"users\" does\n        // not exist` on a fresh DB where auth tables only live in the\n        // auth schema. The libpq `-c search_path=...` option sets the\n        // path at session-start time, before any migration SQL runs,\n        // and survives across the BEGIN/COMMIT cycles MigrationExecutor\n        // uses in `transaction: 'each'` mode.\n        extra: {\n          options: `-c search_path=\"${svc.schema}\",public`,\n        },\n      });\n\n      try {\n        await ds.initialize();\n        // Skip runMigrations() for services with empty migration sets —\n        // gateway-api owns no migrations dir and config-service ships\n        // only `.gitkeep`. Calling runMigrations() with an empty list is\n        // a no-op in TypeORM, but skipping the call avoids creating the\n        // empty `<schema>.migrations` ledger table that would skew the\n        // \"ledger is non-empty\" assertion below.\n        if (migrationClasses.length > 0) {\n          await runWithDbMigrateDdlAuthority(() => ds.runMigrations({ transaction: 'each' }));\n        }\n      } catch (err) {\n        // Surface which service failed so the diagnostic message is\n        // actionable from CI logs. Do not swallow — re-throw so Jest\n        // marks the suite as failed.\n        throw new Error(\n          `[bootstrap-from-scratch] Migration run failed for service \"${svc.name}\" ` +","numLines":75,"startLine":855,"totalLines":1416}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01QX8HfAV1mWxa46w4SKoNei","type":"tool_result","content":"300\t/**\n301\t * Tables moved out of `public` during P6-P9 of the teardown. Each entry\n * is `[tableName, expectedSchema]` and is asserted both directions:\n * the table EXISTS in expectedSchema and DOES NOT exist in public.\n */\nconst MOVED_TABLES: Array<[string, string]> = [\n  // P6 — low-risk singletons\n  ['channel_detection_log', 'sensor'],\n  ['device_tokens', 'notification'],\n  ['weather_settings', 'farm'],\n  ['sentinel_hub_settings', 'farm'],\n  // P7 — moderate\n  ['sensor_type_definitions', 'sensor'],\n  ['feeder_calibrations', 'farm'],\n  ['marine_observations', 'farm'],\n  ['weather_observations', 'farm'],\n  ['notification_logs', 'notification'],\n  // P8 — high-complexity\n  ['tenant_roles', 'auth'],\n  ['employees', 'hr'],\n];\n\n/**\n * Messaging service tables — established in their messaging-schema home\n * by the 2026-04-14 messaging-isolation plan (P7 entity decoration).\n * Asserts each of the 17 messaging entities lives in `messaging` and\n * NOT in `public`. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_0162QeJzTwfHnZ4ypVw2BBSb","type":"tool_result","content":"1228\t  //\n1229\t  // Failure mode this catches: TypeORM \"thought it ran\" but skipped\n1230\t  // migrations. Empirically observed when the migrations array passed\n1231\t  // to TypeOrmModule.forRoot was a stale glob that resolved to fewer\n1232\t  // files than the migrations directory holds, and runMigrations()\n1233\t  // returned without error because every entry it knew about was\n1234\t  // already applied. The ledger row count then drifts below the\n1235\t  // file-system count and silent under-application goes undetected\n1236\t  // until a fresh deploy reveals missing tables.\n1237\t  //\n1238\t  // Assertion: count(<schema>.migrations rows) == count(migration files\n1239\t  // on disk that match the timestamp+PascalCase regex). Skip services\n1240\t  // with empty migration sets (gateway-api, config-service: those have\n1241\t  // NO ledger because we skipped runMigrations() entirely).\n1242\t  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n1243\t  it.each(\n1244\t    SERVICES.filter(\n1245\t      (s) => loadMigrationClassesFromDir(join(REPO_ROOT, s.migrationsDir)).length > 0,\n1246\t    ),\n1247\t  )(\n1248\t    'service \"$name\" migration ledger row count matches on-disk migration file count',\n1249\t    async ({ name, schema, migrationsDir }) => {\n1250\t      const ds = dataSources.get(name);\n1251\t      if (!ds || !ds.isInitialized) {\n1252\t        throw new Error(`${name} DataSource not initialized.`);\n1253\t      }\n1254\t      const onDiskCount = loadMigrationClassesFromDir(join(REPO_ROOT, migrationsDir)).length;\n1255\t      const rows = await ds.query<{ count: string }[]>(\n1256\t        `SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS count FROM \"${schema}\".migrations`,\n1257\t      );\n1258\t      const ledgerCount = parseInt(rows[0]?.count ?? '0', 10);\n1259\t      if (ledgerCount !== onDiskCount) {\n1260\t        throw new Error(\n1261\t          `${schema}.migrations ledger row count drift: on-disk migrations dir has ` +\n1262\t            `${onDiskCount} timestamped files, ledger has ${ledgerCount} rows. ` +\n1263\t            `Either runMigrations() silently skipped some files (TypeORM cache or ` +\n1264\t            `metadata-loader bug) or a file landed on disk without being included ` +\n1265\t            `in the per-service migrations[] manifest.`,\n1266\t        );\n1267\t      }\n1268\t    },\n1269\t  );\n1270\t\n1271\t  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n1272\t  // Part A.1 — Entity-surface coverage matrix."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d5296f0-6d3c-4867-b93d-0a25583fcc68","uuid":"fb0a7a86-481c-4436-bb97-c3bd3745eb81","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:37:27.110Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-0db550b56f62/apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts","content":"  //\n  // Failure mode this catches: TypeORM \"thought it ran\" but skipped\n  // migrations. Empirically observed when the migrations array passed\n  // to TypeOrmModule.forRoot was a stale glob that resolved to fewer\n  // files than the migrations directory holds, and runMigrations()\n  // returned without error because every entry it knew about was\n  // already applied. The ledger row count then drifts below the\n  // file-system count and silent under-application goes undetected\n  // until a fresh deploy reveals missing tables.\n  //\n  // Assertion: count(<schema>.migrations rows) == count(migration files\n  // on disk that match the timestamp+PascalCase regex). Skip services\n  // with empty migration sets (gateway-api, config-service: those have\n  // NO ledger because we skipped runMigrations() entirely).\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  it.each(\n    SERVICES.filter(\n      (s) => loadMigrationClassesFromDir(join(REPO_ROOT, s.migrationsDir)).length > 0,\n    ),\n  )(\n    'service \"$name\" migration ledger row count matches on-disk migration file count',\n    async ({ name, schema, migrationsDir }) => {\n      const ds = dataSources.get(name);\n      if (!ds || !ds.isInitialized) {\n        throw new Error(`${name} DataSource not initialized.`);\n      }\n      const onDiskCount = loadMigrationClassesFromDir(join(REPO_ROOT, migrationsDir)).length;\n      const rows = await ds.query<{ count: string }[]>(\n        `SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS count FROM \"${schema}\".migrations`,\n      );\n      const ledgerCount = parseInt(rows[0]?.count ?? '0', 10);\n      if (ledgerCount !== onDiskCount) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `${schema}.migrations ledger row count drift: on-disk migrations dir has ` +\n            `${onDiskCount} timestamped files, ledger has ${ledgerCount} rows. ` +\n            `Either runMigrations() silently skipped some files (TypeORM cache or ` +\n            `metadata-loader bug) or a file landed on disk without being included ` +\n            `in the per-service migrations[] manifest.`,\n        );\n      }\n    },\n  );\n\n  // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n  // Part A.1 — Entity-surface coverage matrix.","numLines":45,"startLine":1228,"totalLines":1416}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Wh8BsNHLDCvHoXbwVg4Qvi","type":"tool_result","content":"270\t  # GHA runners have docker preinstalled and the testcontainers library\n271\t  # autoreaches it; no extra setup beyond `npm ci`.\n272\t  # ===================================================================\n273\t  bootstrap-from-scratch:\n274\t    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n275\t    # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — bootstrap-from-scratch is now ~8 minutes\n276\t    # because the entity-surface coverage matrix runs per service AND\n277\t    # the SchemaDriftValidator runtime gate (Part A.4) iterates each\n278\t    # service's metadata. 12-minute ceiling gives ~50% headroom while\n279\t    # keeping the zombie-job blast radius bounded.\n280\t    timeout-minutes: 12\n281\t\n282\t    steps:\n283\t      - name: Checkout repository\n284\t        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n285\t\n286\t      - name: Setup Node.js\n287\t        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n288\t        with:\n289\t          node-version: '22'\n290\t          cache: 'npm'\n291\t\n292\t      - name: Install dependencies\n293\t        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n294\t\n295\t      # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — testcontainers image cache extension.\n296\t      # The Postgres pull (~250 MB compressed) dominates cold-start\n297\t      # time; restoring the image tarball from a cache key shaves\n298\t      # 60-90 seconds off every CI run that touches a path-filtered\n299\t      # surface. Cache key bound to the pinned digest in the spec\n300\t      # so a digest bump invalidates the cache (correct behaviour).\n301\t      - name: Restore docker image cache\n302\t        id: docker-image-cache\n303\t        uses: actions/cache@55cc8345863c7cc4c66a329aec7e433d2d1c52a9 # v6.1.0\n304\t        with:\n305\t          path: /tmp/docker-image-cache\n306\t          key: db-migration-check-pg16-timescaledb-${{ hashFiles('apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts') }}\n307\t\n308\t      - name: Load cached image (if present)\n309\t        if: steps.docker-image-cache.outputs.cache-hit == 'true'\n310\t        run: |\n311\t          if [ -f /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar ]; then\n312\t            docker load -i /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar\n313\t          fi\n314\t\n315\t      - name: Run bootstrap-from-scratch test\n316\t        run: npm run test:bootstrap\n317\t        env:\n318\t          # testcontainers reuse cuts seconds off subsequent runs and is\n319\t          # safe in single-job CI (we don't reuse across jobs)."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d5296f0-6d3c-4867-b93d-0a25583fcc68","uuid":"389f16a6-6537-423e-a417-eb3b54f74305","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:37:28.186Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-0db550b56f62/.github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml","content":"  # GHA runners have docker preinstalled and the testcontainers library\n  # autoreaches it; no extra setup beyond `npm ci`.\n  # ===================================================================\n  bootstrap-from-scratch:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — bootstrap-from-scratch is now ~8 minutes\n    # because the entity-surface coverage matrix runs per service AND\n    # the SchemaDriftValidator runtime gate (Part A.4) iterates each\n    # service's metadata. 12-minute ceiling gives ~50% headroom while\n    # keeping the zombie-job blast radius bounded.\n    timeout-minutes: 12\n\n    steps:\n      - name: Checkout repository\n        uses: actions/checkout@3d3c42e5aac5ba805825da76410c181273ba90b1 # v7.0.1\n\n      - name: Setup Node.js\n        uses: actions/setup-node@820762786026740c76f36085b0efc47a31fe5020 # v7.0.0\n        with:\n          node-version: '22'\n          cache: 'npm'\n\n      - name: Install dependencies\n        run: npm ci --prefer-offline --no-audit --ignore-scripts --no-fund\n\n      # Wave 4-A.2 Dalga 5 — testcontainers image cache extension.\n      # The Postgres pull (~250 MB compressed) dominates cold-start\n      # time; restoring the image tarball from a cache key shaves\n      # 60-90 seconds off every CI run that touches a path-filtered\n      # surface. Cache key bound to the pinned digest in the spec\n      # so a digest bump invalidates the cache (correct behaviour).\n      - name: Restore docker image cache\n        id: docker-image-cache\n        uses: actions/cache@55cc8345863c7cc4c66a329aec7e433d2d1c52a9 # v6.1.0\n        with:\n          path: /tmp/docker-image-cache\n          key: db-migration-check-pg16-timescaledb-${{ hashFiles('apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts') }}\n\n      - name: Load cached image (if present)\n        if: steps.docker-image-cache.outputs.cache-hit == 'true'\n        run: |\n          if [ -f /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar ]; then\n            docker load -i /tmp/docker-image-cache/timescaledb-pg16.tar\n          fi\n\n      - name: Run bootstrap-from-scratch test\n        run: npm run test:bootstrap\n        env:\n          # testcontainers reuse cuts seconds off subsequent runs and is\n          # safe in single-job CI (we don't reuse across jobs).","numLines":50,"startLine":270,"totalLines":777}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01M25itUtYtvxW9HJSbL1MeD","type":"tool_result","content":"600\t */\n601\texport async function rollbackSchemaMigrations(\n602\t  opts: RunSchemaOptions,\n603\t  rollback: RollbackSchemaOptions,\n604\t): Promise<RollbackSchemaResult> {\n605\t  const { schema, migrations, entities, log } = opts;\n606\t  const { count } = rollback;\n607\t\n608\t  if (!Number.isInteger(count) || count < 1) {\n609\t    throw new Error(\n610\t      `[db-migrate] Rollback count must be a positive integer; received ${count}.`,\n611\t    );\n612\t  }\n613\t\n614\t  const started = Date.now();\n615\t  log({\n616\t    level: 'warn',\n617\t    message: 'Schema migration rollback starting',\n618\t    context: 'DbMigrate',\n619\t    schema,\n620\t    count,\n621\t    migrationsGlobs: migrations,\n622\t    ...(entities !== undefined ? { entitiesGlobs: entities } : {}),\n623\t  });\n624\t\n625\t  return withLockedMigrationSession(opts, async ({ queryRunner, executor }) => {\n626\t    const executed = await executor.getExecutedMigrations();\n627\t    if (count > executed.length) {\n628\t      throw new Error(\n629\t        `[db-migrate] Cannot roll back ${count} migration(s) for \"${schema}\" ` +\n630\t          `because only ${executed.length} executed migration(s) exist.`,\n631\t      );\n632\t    }\n633\t\n634\t    const reverted: string[] = [];\n635\t    for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {\n636\t      await queryRunner.query(`SET search_path TO \"${schema}\", public`);\n637\t      const before = await executor.getExecutedMigrations();\n638\t      const migration = before[0];\n639\t      if (!migration?.name) {\n640\t        throw new Error(\n641\t          `[db-migrate] Could not identify migration ${i + 1} selected for rollback on \"${schema}\".`,\n642\t        );\n643\t      }\n644\t      await queryRunner.startTransaction();\n645\t      try {\n646\t        await setMigrationExecutionContext(queryRunner, migration.name, 'down');\n647\t        await executor.undoLastMigration();\n648\t        if (queryRunner.isTransactionActive) {\n649\t          await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n650\t        }\n651\t      } catch (err: unknown) {\n652\t        if (queryRunner.isTransactionActive) {\n653\t          await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n654\t        }\n655\t        throw err;\n656\t      }\n657\t      reverted.push(migration.name);\n658\t      log({\n659\t        level: 'warn',\n660\t        message: 'Migration reverted',\n661\t        context: 'DbMigrate',\n662\t        schema,\n663\t        migration: migration?.name ?? '<unknown>',\n664\t      });\n665\t    }\n666\t\n667\t    log({\n668\t      level: 'warn',\n669\t      message: 'Schema migration rollback complete',\n670\t      context: 'DbMigrate',\n671\t      schema,\n672\t      reverted,\n673\t    });\n674\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0d5296f0-6d3c-4867-b93d-0a25583fcc68","uuid":"455093a2-54eb-41e7-b0a4-8eb19aecd152","timestamp":"2026-09-19T15:38:23.580Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-0db550b56f62/apps/db-migrate/src/migration-orchestrator.ts","content":" */\nexport async function rollbackSchemaMigrations(\n  opts: RunSchemaOptions,\n  rollback: RollbackSchemaOptions,\n): Promise<RollbackSchemaResult> {\n  const { schema, migrations, entities, log } = opts;\n  const { count } = rollback;\n\n  if (!Number.isInteger(count) || count < 1) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `[db-migrate] Rollback count must be a positive integer; received ${count}.`,\n    );\n  }\n\n  const started = Date.now();\n  log({\n    level: 'warn',\n    message: 'Schema migration rollback starting',\n    context: 'DbMigrate',\n    schema,\n    count,\n    migrationsGlobs: migrations,\n    ...(entities !== undefined ? { entitiesGlobs: entities } : {}),\n  });\n\n  return withLockedMigrationSession(opts, async ({ queryRunner, executor }) => {\n    const executed = await executor.getExecutedMigrations();\n    if (count > executed.length) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `[db-migrate] Cannot roll back ${count} migration(s) for \"${schema}\" ` +\n          `because only ${executed.length} executed migration(s) exist.`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    const reverted: string[] = [];\n    for (let i = 0; i < count; i += 1) {\n      await queryRunner.query(`SET search_path TO \"${schema}\", public`);\n      const before = await executor.getExecutedMigrations();\n      const migration = before[0];\n      if (!migration?.name) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `[db-migrate] Could not identify migration ${i + 1} selected for rollback on \"${schema}\".`,\n        );\n      }\n      await queryRunner.startTransaction();\n      try {\n        await setMigrationExecutionContext(queryRunner, migration.name, 'down');\n        await executor.undoLastMigration();\n        if (queryRunner.isTransactionActive) {\n          await queryRunner.commitTransaction();\n        }\n      } catch (err: unknown) {\n        if (queryRunner.isTransactionActive) {\n          await queryRunner.rollbackTransaction();\n        }\n        throw err;\n      }\n      reverted.push(migration.name);\n      log({\n        level: 'warn',\n        message: 'Migration reverted',\n        context: 'DbMigrate',\n        schema,\n        migration: migration?.name ?? '<unknown>',\n      });\n    }\n\n    log({\n      level: 'warn',\n      message: 'Schema migration rollback complete',\n      context: 'DbMigrate',\n      schema,\n      reverted,\n    });\n","numLines":75,"startLine":600,"totalLines":682}}}

The\n    # request can opt out via `allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix=True`\n    # for the rare judge-only domain that explicitly does not produce\n    # per-criterion evidence — operator-approval-gated; not a default.\n    matrix = response.get(\"satisfaction_matrix\") or []\n    allow_empty_matrix = bool(request and request.get(\"allow_empty_satisfaction_matrix\"))\n    if not matrix and not allow_empty_matrix:\n        errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_satisfaction_matrix_must_be_non_empty\"})\n    if isinstance(matrix, list):\n        for entry in matrix:\n            if not isinstance(entry, dict):\n                continue\n            entry_refs = entry.get(\"evidence_refs\") or []\n            if not isinstance(entry_refs, list):\n                errors.append({\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_refs_not_list\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")})\n                continue\n            for ref in entry_refs:\n                if not isinstance(ref, str) or not ref.strip():\n                    errors.append(\n                        {\"code\": \"agent_matrix_evidence_ref_not_string\", \"id\": entry.get(\"id\")}\n                    )\n                    continue\n                _check_agent_ref(\n                    ref, root=root, errors=errors, checked=checked,\n                    allow_kernel_artifacts=allow_kernel_artifacts,\n                )\n                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(ref):\n                    continue  # Z2 — same single definition as above\n                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(ref, root)[0]:\n                    continue  # ORPHAN-734 — same single definition as above\n                envelope = classify_evidence_ref(\n                    ref,\n                    workspace_root=root,\n                    source_hint=\"agent_output\",\n                    target_sha=_request_target_sha(request),\n                    probe_session=probe_session,\n                )\n                evidence_envelopes.append(envelope.to_dict())\n                try:\n                    EvidencePolicy.require_repo_verified(envelope)\n                except GovernanceError as exc:\n                    errors.append({\n                        \"code\": _unverified_evidence_code(\"agent\", envelope.trust_grade),\n                        \"ref\": ref,\n                        \"reason\": str(exc),\n                    })\n\n    # Refs\n    # outside the box are rejected as scope leakage.\n    #\n    # Plan 024 §B-2 — allowed_scope MUST be provided when a request is\n    # supplied. Pre-fix the empty-list case skipped scope enforcement\n    # entirely under \"preserves backward compatibility with legacy\n    # aria/agent-invocation-request/v1 rows that pre-date Plan 016\".\n    # That path is now closed: legacy rows are caught at\n    # _strict_request_view (line ~964); by the time validate_agent_-\n    # response_evidence sees the request the strict fields are\n    # present.\n    if request is not None:\n        if \"allowed_scope\" not in request:\n            errors.append({\"code\": \"evidence_request_missing_allowed_scope\"})\n        else:\n            allowed_globs = list(request.get(\"allowed_scope\") or [])\n            allowed_request_refs = {\n                (_parse_agent_ref(r) or (\"\", None))[0]\n                for r in (request.get(\"evidence_refs\") or [])\n                if isinstance(r, str)\n            }\n            for path in checked:\n                if _is_ledger_pointer_ref(path):\n                    continue  # Z2 — pointer identity is bound at mint, not by glob\n                if allow_kernel_artifacts and _kernel_artifact_verdict(path, root)[0]:\n                    # ORPHAN-734 — an arbitrated artifact's identity is bound\n                    # by the results ledger's content hash, not by a repo glob\n                    # (the same reason the pointer above skips this check).\n                    continue\n                if path in allowed_request_refs:\n                    continue\n                if not _path_matches_any_glob(path, allowed_globs):\n                    errors.append(\n                        {\n                            \"code\": \"agent_evidence_outside_allowed_scope\",\n                            \"path\": path,\n                            \"allowed_scope\": allowed_globs,\n                        }\n                    )\n\n    return {\n        \"valid\": not errors,\n        \"errors\": errors,\n        \"checked_refs\": sorted(set(checked)),\n        \"evidence_envelopes\": evidence_envelopes,\n    }\n\n\ndef _request_target_sha(request: dict[str, Any] | None) -> str | None:\n    if not request:","numLines":110,"startLine":612,"totalLines":774}}}

The adapter's literal import-graph statement is accurate: no spec shares the basename 1800000000000-Baseline, none sits in apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations or a __tests__ under it, none statically imports the file, and the only sibling spec (apps/hr-service/src/database/__tests__/sync-hr-entities-to-db.spec.ts:2,23) targets an archived migration and imports nothing. The substantive claim the finding asserts at HIGH severity — an untested hazardous migration — is contradicted by repository content: apps/db-migrate/src/__tests__/bootstrap-from-scratch.spec.ts lists the hr migrations directory in its SERVICES manifest (:248-253), loads every <13-digit>-<PascalCase>.ts in it via readdirSync + createRequire (:468-494; the Baseline matches and exports Baseline1800000000000), executes the chain with ds.runMigrations on a fresh Testcontainers Postgres (:861-923), asserts the hr.migrations ledger count equals the on-disk file count (:1243-1269), and requires RLS policies on hr tables (:371-379) — the effect of the Baseline's applyTenantRlsToSchema call (1800000000000-Baseline.ts:242-245). That spec is CI-gated (package.json:125 test:bootstrap; .github/workflows/db-migration-check.yml:273-316, path-triggered by apps/*/src/**/migrations/** at :91). The same workflow runs the real aqua-db-migrate with hr credentials (:398-424, :409), provisions a real tenant through the production provisioner (:443, which replays tenant-aware Baselines), then runs e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts (:474), which asserts employees lives in hr (:319). tests/invariants/tenant-aware-migration-ddl-guard.spec.ts reads the Baseline text via git ls-files (:52, :264-276; it is not in the skip list at :282) and asserts DDL-routing properties on it. The hazard tokens that triggered tools/aria-adapters/test-gap-adapter.ts:274-275 are DROP statements confined to down() (Baseline :282-329), DELETE tokens inside FK ON DELETE clauses (:212-239) and the REVOKE UPDATE, DELETE hardening (:262); matchingTests (:296-320) cannot see directory-scan or git-ls-files loaders, which is why the signal fired. Residual surfaces no test exercises: the Baseline's down() (a live path via apps/db-migrate/src/migration-orchestrator.ts:601-657 rollbackSchemaMigrations -> undoLastMigration at :647) and the runtime behaviour of the payroll_audit append-only trigger (:248-263).    },\n    \"teaching_note\": \"What had to be done: decide whether the hr Baseline migration really lacks test coverage, not whether a heuristic says so. Why it matters: a HIGH migration_without_test label routes implementer effort toward writing a spec for a file the CI pipeline already executes on every migration-touching PR; mislabelling it also teaches the adapter's precision metric the wrong lesson. What breaks if skipped: the goldset would record a coverage gap that is an artefact of how the loaders work (readdirSync + createRequire, git ls-files) rather than a real hole, while the genuine narrower gap — the Baseline's down() rollback path and the payroll_audit trigger behaviour — would stay unnamed. Downstream surface: the consensus arbiter's TP/FP tally for test-gap-adapter and any plan minted from this finding.
